  1. [2610.33084] Hahn微积分中的空间曲线：旋转结构、基本定理与不动点处的q变形不变量
    Space curves in Hahn calculus: rotation structure, a fundamental theorem, and $q$-deformed invariants at the fixed point
    👥 Umut Selvi, Derya Sağlam
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Hahn量子微积分中空间曲线的微分几何，定义Hahn Frenet标架并证明基本定理。
    • 通过旋转无限乘积和Jackson-Nörlund积分建立曲线由迹和收缩决定。
    • 发现不动点处曲率和挠率带有q变形因子，并分析其几何意义与极限行为。
    • 刻画广义Hahn螺线，指出常曲率挠率曲线不构成螺线，给出唯一性结果。
    💡 在Hahn量子微积分中建立空间曲线微分几何，证明基本定理并揭示不动点处曲率挠率的q变形因子。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们发展Hahn量子微积分中的空间曲线微分几何，其中导数是沿收缩σ(t)=qt+ω的差商，不动点为ω0=ω/(1-q)。Hahn Frenet标架沿σ的每条轨道通过旋转（转角与挠角）传输，这将其等同于内接轨道多边形的离散Frenet标架。嵌入正则Hahn曲线在ω0附近的线性重参数化下由迹和诱导收缩唯一确定。我们证明了一个尖锐的平面性判据和一个通过旋转的无限乘积与Jackson-Nörlund积分表述的基本定理。对于光滑曲线，关于q一致的展开表明在ω0处Hahn曲率和挠率分别带有因子[2]_q/2和[3]_q/3。这些因子是几何衰减步长的采样效应，在边长归一化下消失，但它们的商持续存在于每个内接于光滑曲线（顶点单调收敛且边长以比率q衰减）的多边形的挠角与转角之比的极限中。在向稳定结点的一致采样松弛中，这些因子可能成为可测偏差。我们用转角与挠角之间的两步关系刻画广义Hahn螺线；其在ω0处的Lancret比为±([2]_q/2)cotϑ，其中ϑ是螺线角。与经典情形不同，挠率与曲率的常数比不能刻画螺线：常曲率和常挠率的曲线从不是广义Hahn螺线，且在ω0附近，给定比率的单位速度螺线在相似和线性重参数化下唯一。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We develop the differential geometry of space curves in Hahn's quantum calculus, where the derivative is the difference quotient along the contraction $\sigma(t)=qt+\omega$ with fixed point $\omega_0=\omega/(1-q)$. The Hahn Frenet frame is transported along every orbit of $\sigma$ by rotations through turning and torsion angles, which identifies it with the discrete Frenet frame of the inscribed orbit polygon. An embedded regular Hahn curve is determined, up to linear reparametrization about $\omega_0$, by its trace and the induced contraction. We prove a sharp planarity criterion and a fundamental theorem via an infinite product of rotations and the Jackson--N\"orlund integral. For smooth curves, expansions uniform in $q$ show that at $\omega_0$ the Hahn curvature and torsion carry the factors $[2]_q/2$ and $[3]_q/3$. These factors, a sampling effect of geometrically decaying steps, disappear under edge-length normalization, but their quotient persists in the limiting ratio of torsion and turning angles of every polygon inscribed in a smooth curve with monotonically converging vertices and edge lengths decaying with ratio~$q$. In uniformly sampled relaxation towards a stable node, the factors can become measurable biases. We characterize generalized Hahn helices by a two-step relation between turning and torsion angles; their Lancret ratio at $\omega_0$ is $\pm([2]_q/2)\cot\vartheta$, where $\vartheta$ is the helix angle. Unlike the classical case, a constant ratio of torsion and curvature does not characterize helices: curves with constant curvature and torsion are never generalized Hahn helices, and, near $\omega_0$, unit-speed helices with a given ratio are unique up to similarities and linear reparametrization.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33084
  2. [2610.33090] 奇异Atiyah构型
    Singular Atiyah Configurations
    👥 Jan Eickhoff
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究欧氏三维空间中Atiyah多项式线性相关的点构型问题。
    • 构造由两个同轴循环轨道和轴点组成的任意大构型，包括平面情形。
    • 将构造推广到双曲三维空间，得到类似结果。
    💡 构造任意大的不同点构型使其Atiyah多项式线性相关，包括平面和双曲空间情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们构造了欧几里得三维空间中任意大的不同点构型，使其Atiyah多项式线性相关，包括平面构型。这些构型由两个同轴循环轨道和轴点组成。我们还在双曲三维空间中得到类似的构型。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We construct arbitrarily large configurations of distinct points in Euclidean three-space with linearly dependent Atiyah polynomials, including planar configurations. The configurations consist of two coaxial cyclic orbits and axial points. We also obtain analogous configurations in hyperbolic three-space.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33090
  3. [2610.33261] 极小超曲面Besse族的刚性及形变
    Rigidity and deformations of Besse families of minimal hypersurfaces
    👥 Christian Lange
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 将Zoll族推广为Besse族，用接触几何刻画为双Legendrian纤维化。
    • 证明球面（除4维）上Besse族超曲面正则，偶数维球面（除4维）上更强为正则嵌入。
    • 构造非恒定曲率度量上的非嵌入极小超曲面Besse族及orbifold上的例外例子。
    • 给出Zoll族流形同胚于球面或实射影空间的简短证明。
    💡 推广Zoll族为Besse族，证明球面（除4维）上Besse族超曲面正则，并构造非嵌入极小超曲面例子。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们将超曲面的Zoll族推广为Besse族，后者用微分拓扑定义，可能包含非嵌入和例外超曲面。通过接触几何讨论将其呈现为双Legendrian纤维化，并证明正则超曲面要么是球面要么是实射影空间。类比于关于所有测地线闭的単连通流形的Berger猜想，我们证明球面（除4维球面）上Besse族的每个超曲面都是正则的。在偶数维球面（除4维球面）上我们得到更强结论，推广了Gromoll和Grove关于所有测地线闭的2维球面的定理：Besse族的每个超曲面都是正则且嵌入的。此外，基于Ambrozio和Guajardo的工作，我们在每个至少3的奇数维提供非恒定曲率度量携带非嵌入极小超曲面的Besse族，并在每个至少2的维数提供orbifold上带例外超曲面的极小超曲面Besse族。我们还给出一个简短证明：承认嵌入超曲面Zoll族的流形同胚于球面或实射影空间，在同胚意义下恢复Martins的近期结果。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We generalize Zoll families of hypersurfaces to Besse families of hypersurfaces, which are defined differential-topologically and may contain non-embedded and exceptional hypersurfaces. A contact-geometric discussion presents them as double Legendrian fibrations and shows that regular hypersurfaces are either spheres or real projective spaces. In analogy with the Berger conjecture on simply connected manifolds all of whose geodesics are closed, we show that every hypersurface of a Besse family on a sphere other than the 4-sphere is regular. On even-dimensional spheres other than the 4-sphere we obtain a stronger conclusion, generalizing a theorem of Gromoll and Grove about 2-spheres all of whose geodesics are closed: every hypersurface of a Besse family is regular and embedded. Moreover, building on work by Ambrozio and Guajardo, we provide metrics of non-constant curvature carrying Besse families of non-embedded minimal hypersurfaces in every odd dimension at least three, and Besse families of minimal hypersurfaces with exceptional hypersurfaces on orbifolds in every dimension at least two. We also give a short proof that manifolds admitting Zoll families of embedded hypersurfaces are homeomorphic to spheres or real projective spaces, recovering a recent result of Martins up to homeomorphism.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33261
  4. [2610.33417] 面积有界PGL3-Hitchin模空间的体积
    Volume of area-bounded PGL3-Hitchin moduli space
    👥 Qiongling Li, Beibei Liu, Weixu Su, Zhe Sun
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究PGL3-Hitchin模空间中Hilbert-Busemann面积有界子集的辛体积。
    • 证明该子集的Goldman辛体积具有多项式上界，并给出加权体积的显式估计。
    💡 研究PGL3-Hitchin模空间中Hilbert-Busemann面积有界子集，给出Goldman辛体积上界及加权体积估计。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于曲面S_{g,m}（m>0）上固定边界数据L的无标记凸RP^2结构的PGL3-Hitchin模空间，我们研究其规范Hilbert-Busemann面积上界为t的子集。我们证明该子集的Goldman辛体积至多为C_{g,m}(t+λ^2)^{8g-8+3m}，其中λ是m>0时最大总根边界长度，m=0时设为0。作为推论，我们得到e^{-s·Area}加权体积的显式上界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For the $\mathrm{PGL}_3$-Hitchin moduli space of unmarked convex $\mathbb{RP}^2$ structures on the surface $S_{g,m}$ with fixed boundary data $\mathbf L$ when $m>0$, we investigate the subset whose canonical Hilbert--Busemann area is bounded above by $t$. We show that the Goldman symplectic volume of this subset is at most $C_{g,m}(t+\lambda^2)^{8g-8+3m}$, where $\lambda$ is the largest total-root boundary length for $m>0$ and is set to $0$ for $m=0$. As a consequence, We obtain the explicit upper bound for the $e^{-s\cdot\mathrm{Area}}$-weighted volume.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33417
  5. [2610.33474] Hitchin α不变量与同伦9维球面上的正截面曲率
    Hitchin's $\alpha$-invariant and positive sectional curvature on homotopy $9$-spheres
    👥 Yang-Hui He, Ziran Liu, Shing-Tung Yau
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究同伦9维球面上正截面曲率度量的存在性与Hitchin α不变量的关系。
    • 证明正截面曲率度量存在当且仅当α不变量为零，等价于正标量曲率度量存在。
    💡 证明光滑同伦9维球面承认正截面曲率度量当且仅当Hitchin α不变量为零，等价于承认正标量曲率度量。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明一个光滑同伦9维球面承认正截面曲率的黎曼度量当且仅当其Hitchin α不变量为零，等价地当且仅当它承认正标量曲率的黎曼度量。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that a smooth homotopy $9$-sphere admits a Riemannian metric of positive sectional curvature if and only if its Hitchin $\alpha$-invariant vanishes, or equivalently if and only if it admits a Riemannian metric of positive scalar curvature.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33474
  6. [2610.33721] 次黎曼流形上的多边际最优传输
    Multi-marginal optimal transport on sub-Riemannian manifolds
    👥 Haowei Lin
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究理想次黎曼流形上的多边际最优传输问题。
    • 建立次黎曼重心平衡条件，在附加假设下证明Monge问题解的存在性。
    • 研究Wasserstein重心的绝对连续性，推广Heisenberg群上的结果并回答两个公开问题。
    💡 研究理想次黎曼流形上的多边际最优传输，建立重心平衡条件，证明Monge问题解存在性及Wasserstein重心绝对连续性。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究理想次黎曼流形上的多边际最优传输。我们建立了次黎曼重心平衡条件，并在附加假设下证明Monge问题解的存在性。我们还研究了Wasserstein重心的绝对连续性。我们的结果扩展并改进了Pass、Pinamonti和Vedovato（Methods Appl. Anal. 28 (2021), 61-76）在Heisenberg群上的工作，回答了其提出的两个公开问题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we study multi-marginal optimal transport on ideal sub-Riemannian manifolds. We establish a sub-Riemannian barycentric balance condition and prove the existence of a solution to the Monge problem under an additional assumption. We also investigate the absolute continuity of Wasserstein barycenters. Our results extend and improve upon those of Pass, Pinamonti, and Vedovato (Methods Appl. Anal. 28 (2021), 61-76) on the Heisenberg group, answering two open questions raised in their work.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33721
  7. [2610.33812] 1+4维次极端Myers-Perry时空上标量波的Price定律
    Price's law for scalar waves on 1+4 dimensional subextremal Myers-Perry spacetimes
    👥 Pieter-Bart Peters
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究1+4维次极端Myers-Perry时空上标量波方程的晚期渐近行为。
    • 证明Hamilton流在次极端范围内法向双曲捕获，并在实模态稳定假设下导出Price定律。
    • 得到解按t^{-3}衰减的精确渐近，首项系数可显式计算。
    💡 证明1+4维次极端Myers-Perry时空Hamilton流法向双曲捕获，并在实模态稳定假设下得到标量波Price定律φ=ct^{-3}+O(t^{-4+})。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们考虑1+4维具有两个自旋参数(a,b)∈R^2的Myers-Perry时空(M,g_{a,b,m})，它是1+3维著名Kerr时空的直接推广。我们证明与g_{a,b,m}^{-1}相关的Hamilton流在整个次极端范围(|a|+|b|)^2<2m内是法向双曲捕获的。作为应用，我们在实模态稳定假设下证明标量波方程□_{g_{a,b,m}}φ=0的解φ(t,x)的Price定律，具有显式首项。我们的精确渐近蕴含φ(t,x)=ct^{-3}+O(t^{-4+})，其中c∈C是可显式计算常数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We consider Myers--Perry spacetimes $(M,g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}})$ in $1+4$ dimensions with two spin parameters $(\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b})\in\mathbb{R}^2$, which are a direct generalization of the well known Kerr spacetime in $1+3$ dimensions. We prove that the Hamiltonian flow associated to $g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}}^{-1}$ is normally hyperbolically trapped for the full subextremal range \[ (|\mathbf{a}|+|\mathbf{b}|)^2<2\mathfrak{m}. \] As an application, we prove a Price law with explicit leading order terms for solutions $\phi(t,x)$ of the scalar wave equation \[ \Box_{g_{\mathbf{a},\mathbf{b},\mathfrak{m}}}\phi=0 \] under the assumption of real mode stability. Our precise asymptotics imply that $\phi(t,x)=ct^{-3}+\mathcal{O}(t^{-4+})$, where $c\in\mathbb{C}$ is an explicitly computable constant.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33812
  8. [2610.33943] 通过射线最近点投影的外在曲率估计
    Extrinsic Curvature Estimation by Closest Point Projection of Rays
    👥 Liangchen Liu, Yen-Hsi Richard Tsai
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 提出无参数化框架，利用短随机射线的最近点投影估计子流形曲率。
    • 分析诱导曲线几何，刻画其为测地线的条件，并给出超曲面的两个带误差界算法。
    • 推广到高余维和点云情形，实验验证了理论速率。
    💡 提出无参数化框架，利用短随机射线的最近点投影估计子流形第二基本形式和主曲率，并给出误差界与算法。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们提出一个无参数化框架，用于估计R^d中子流形的第二基本形式和主曲率。该方法利用少数短随机射线的最近点投影，从这些投影在子流形上诱导的曲线恢复曲率，无需坐标卡或局部拟合。我们分析诱导曲线的几何并刻画其何时为测地线。对于超曲面，这些结果给出两个带误差界的算法：从诱导曲线曲率最小二乘重建第二基本形式，以及从由d条射线构造的d×d矩阵的特征值直接估计所有主曲率，后者无需法信息且除投影外代价为O(d^3)。在更高余维中，我们也能逐个法方向恢复第二基本形式。在点云上，投影变为最近邻查询，我们界定了额外误差。实验证实了超曲面在高达128维、余维高达98的曲面以及点云上的预测速率，展示了该框架的实际有效性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We propose a parametrization-free framework for estimating the second fundamental form and the principal curvatures of a submanifold of $\mathbb{R}^d$. The method uses the closest point projections of a few short random rays and recovers the curvature from the curves that these projections induce on the submanifold, without coordinate charts or local fitting. We analyze the geometry of the induced curves and characterize when they are geodesics. For hypersurfaces, these results give two algorithms with error bounds: a least squares reconstruction of the second fundamental form from the curvatures of the induced curves, and a direct estimate of all principal curvatures from the eigenvalues of a $d\times d$ matrix built from $d$ rays, which needs no normal information and costs $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ besides the projections. In higher codimension, we can also recover the second fundamental form one normal direction at a time. On point clouds, where the projection becomes a nearest neighbor query, we bound the additional error. Experiments confirm the predicted rates for hypersurfaces in up to $128$ dimensions, for surfaces of codimension up to $98$, and on point clouds, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed framework in practice.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33943
  9. [2610.33986] 关于σ_k极大共形体积的注记
    A note on $\sigma_k$ maximal conformal volume
    👥 Mijia Lai
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究σ_k极大共形体积Λ_k的球面上界。
    • 证明在k>n/2且存在k容许度量时，Λ_k不超过标准球面体积，等号仅对标准球面成立。
    • 结合已有存在紧性定理，完成σ_k-Yamabe问题的极大共形体积方法。
    💡 证明Gursky-Viaclovsky引入的σ_k极大共形体积Λ_k的球面上界，等号成立当且仅当共形等价于标准球面。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们建立了Gursky和Viaclovsky引入的σ_k极大共形体积Λ_k的尖锐球面上界。设(M^n,[g])是光滑闭连通共形流形，n≥3且n/2<k≤n，假设[g]包含k容许度量，我们证明Λ_k(M,[g])≤Vol(S^n,g_rd)，等号成立当且仅当(M,[g])共形等价于标准球面。结合Gursky和Viaclovsky在球面阈值以下的存在性和紧性定理，这在k>n/2范围内通过极大共形体积完成了他们处理σ_k-Yamabe问题的方法。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish a sharp spherical upper bound for the $\sigma_k$-maximal conformal volume $\Lambda_k$ introduced by Gursky and Viaclovsky. Let $(M^n,[g])$ be a smooth, closed, connected conformal manifold with $n\geq 3$ and $n/2<k\leq n$, and suppose that $[g]$ contains a $k$-admissible metric, we prove that $ \Lambda_k(M,[g]) \leq \operatorname{Vol}(\mathbb S^n,g_{\mathrm{rd}}), $ with equality if and only if $(M,[g])$ is conformally equivalent to the standard sphere. Combined with the existence and compactness theorem of Gursky and Viaclovsky below the spherical threshold, this completes their approach to the $\sigma_k$-Yamabe problem through maximal conformal volume in the range $k>n/2$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33986
  10. [2610.34036] Loewner和Carathéodory猜想光滑反例的简单构造
    A simple construction of smooth counterexamples to the Loewner and Carath\'eodory conjectures
    👥 Naoya Ando, Masaaki Umehara
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 给出Loewner和Carathéodory猜想光滑反例的更简单构造，以三角函数为种子。
    • 构造平面上Hessian特征线场具有任意指标孤立奇点的函数，并实现为曲面脐点。
    • 利用快速衰减函数得到恰有一个指标2脐点的严格凸闭光滑曲面。
    💡 基于三角函数给出Loewner和Carathéodory猜想光滑反例的更简单构造，并实现任意指标脐点及唯一脐点的凸曲面。
    📖 中文摘要

    Alpöge最近宣布了Loewner和Carathéodory猜想的光滑反例。我们提出一个基于三角函数作为种子的更简单构造。利用此，对每个正整数k，我们构造平面上在原点平坦的光滑实值函数，其Hessian特征线场具有指标为1+k/2的孤立奇点。支撑函数参数化将此指标实现为欧氏三维空间中非实解析C^∞正则曲面芽的脐点指标。同一框架应用于全平面上的快速衰减函数，给出恰有一个指标为2的脐点的严格凸闭光滑曲面。本文关于指标2脐点的存在性和Carathéodory猜想反例的构造是自包含的。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Alp\"oge recently announced smooth counterexamples to the Loewner and Carath\'eodory conjectures. We present a simpler construction based on a trigonometric function as a seed. Using this, for every positive integer $k$, we construct a smooth real-valued function on the plane, flat at the origin, whose Hessian eigenline fields have an isolated singularity of index $1+k/2$. A support-function parametrization realizes this index as an umbilic index of a non-real analytic $C^\infty$-regular surface germ in Euclidean $3$-space. The same framework, applied to a rapidly decreasing function on the whole plane, gives a strictly convex closed smooth surface with exactly one umbilic, of index $2$. This paper is self-contained with regard to the existence of an umbilic of index $2$ and the construction of a counterexample to the Carath\'eodory conjecture.

We also prove that both the stress-energy tensor and the tension field converge strongly in $L^2$ to those of the body map, by ruling out concentration of their $L^2$ energy near the singular points. The distributions are defined by a fixed orthogonal change of the spacelike frame. The equation in the orthogonal-complement direction forces every left-invariant solution to be steady. The remaining equations yield elementary parameter families and finitely many real algebraic branches: four normalized branches for $G_2$, six branches for each sign parameter in $G_4$, and two quartic branches in $G_7$ in addition to its elementary families. Resultants, subresultants, exact polynomial reduction and Sturm root isolation establish necessity, sufficiency and exhaustiveness. Degenerate cases are treated separately, including all families with nonunique soliton vector fields. An invertible frame transformation relates the two distribution problems. The classification is in the stated structure parameters; no quotient by isometries, automorphisms or scaling is taken.

    The quermassintegral inequalities for k-convex spiral-star-shaped domains
    👥 Junfang Li
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究quermassintegral不等式在更广区域类上的推广。
    • 引入由共形Killing场X+AX刻画的螺旋星形区域，包含通常星形区域。

    • 利用正螺旋支撑函数控制非通常星形初始超曲面。

    💡 将quermassintegral不等式从星形区域推广到螺旋星形区域，并证明局部约束逆曲率流的长时间存在与收敛。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们将先前工作（Guan-Li 2009）中的 quermassintegral 不等式从星形区域推广到欧氏空间中一个显著更广的区域类，称为螺旋星形。一个光滑区域称为螺旋星形，若其边界满足 ⟨X+AX,ν⟩>0，其中 A 是固定反对称线性映射，X 是位置向量，ν 是外单位法向。向量场 X+AX 是共形Killing场，膨胀分量为 X、旋转分量为 AX；其轨道结合膨胀与旋转，故称螺旋。当 A=0 时退化为通常星形性，非零 A 允许在通常径向意义下非星形的区域。对光滑 k-凸螺旋星形区域，我们证明一个局部约束逆曲率流的长时间存在性及指数收敛到圆球。正螺旋支撑函数提供处理不必通常星形的初始超曲面所需的几何控制。沿流的 quermassintegral 单调性在此扩大类中给出尖锐 quermassintegral 不等式，等号成立当且仅当区域为球。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We extend the quermassintegral inequalities of our earlier work \cite{GuanLi2009} from star-shaped domains to a substantially broader class of domains in Euclidean space, which we call spiral-star-shaped. A smooth domain is spiral-star-shaped if its boundary satisfies \[ \langle X+A X,\nu\rangle>0 \] for some fixed skew-symmetric linear map $ A$, where $X$ is the position vector and $\nu$ is the outward unit normal. The vector field $X+A X$ is conformal Killing, with dilation component $X$ and rotational component $A X$; its trajectories combine expansion and rotation, giving rise to the spiral terminology. When $A=0$, it reduces to ordinary star-shapedness, while nonzero $A$ allows domains that are not star-shaped in the usual radial sense. For smooth $k$-convex spiral-star-shaped domains, we prove long-time existence and exponential convergence to a round sphere for a locally constrained inverse curvature flow. The positive spiral support function provides the geometric control needed to treat initial hypersurfaces that need not be star-shaped in the usual sense. Monotonicity of the quermassintegrals along the flow yields sharp quermassintegral inequalities in this enlarged class, with equality if and only if the domain is a ball.

  28. [2610.35492] Weil-Petersson体积的一致大亏格渐近
    Uniform large-genus asymptotics of Weil-Petersson volumes
    👥 Zhiyi Li, Qihang Wang
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究Weil-Petersson体积在亏格趋于无穷时的渐近行为。
    • 对任意尖点数n给出按亏格倒幂的一致渐近展开，覆盖n达到√g量级的情形。
    • 截断到√g阶可得指数小相对误差，并确定Mirzakhani-Zograf常数为1/√π。
    • 回答Mirzakhani关于体积比与系数比一致渐近轮廓的两个问题。
    • 证明基于混合κ1-ψ交数的正递推与纯ψ交数渐近的比较。
    💡 对任意尖点数n给出亏格趋于无穷时Weil-Petersson体积的一致渐近展开，并确定Mirzakhani-Zograf常数。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 V_{g,n} 表示亏格 g、n 个尖点的双曲曲面模空间的 Weil-Petersson 体积。我们得到当 g→∞ 时按亏格倒幂的渐近展开，且对所有 n≥0 一致。这包括尖点数达到或超过 √g 量级的情形，此时 Mirzakhani 与 Zograf 的固定 n 展开不再给出均匀逼近。首项显式，相对误差为 O(g^{-1})。将展开截断到与 √g 成正比的适当阶数，得到相对误差 O(exp(-a√g))（某 a>0），且对 n 的增长无限制。对固定 n，首项将 Mirzakhani-Zograf 渐近中的普适常数确定为 1/√π，即 Zograf 的猜想。我们还回答 Mirzakhani 的两个问题：确定在尖点数上一致的体积比渐近轮廓，以及在所有次数上一致的单边界体积多项式相邻系数比。证明使用混合 κ1-ψ 交数的正递推，绝对归一化通过与纯 ψ 交数已知渐近比较确定。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $V_{g,n}$ denote the Weil--Petersson volume of the moduli space of genus-$g$ hyperbolic surfaces with $n$ cusps. We obtain an asymptotic expansion in inverse powers of the genus as $g\to\infty$, uniformly for all $n\ge0$. This includes cusp counts of order $\sqrt g$ and larger, where the fixed-$n$ expansion of Mirzakhani and Zograf no longer gives a uniform approximation. The leading term is explicit and has relative error $O(g^{-1})$. Truncating the expansion at a suitably chosen order proportional to $\sqrt g$ gives relative error $O(\exp(-a\sqrt g))$ for some $a>0$, with no restriction on the growth of $n$. For fixed $n$, the leading term identifies the universal constant in the Mirzakhani--Zograf asymptotics as $1/\sqrt\pi$, as conjectured by Zograf. We also answer two questions of Mirzakhani: we determine explicit asymptotic profiles for volume ratios uniformly in the number of cusps, and for adjacent coefficient ratios of the one-boundary volume polynomial uniformly over all degrees. The proof uses a positive recursion for mixed $\kappa_1$--$\psi$ intersection numbers, with the absolute normalization determined by comparison with the known asymptotics of pure $\psi$ intersection numbers.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35492

On a closed manifold the canonical foliation is automatically Riemannian: a nonzero period obstructs the exact twist required by the non-Riemannian models. The compact structures form four families, distinguished by $b_1=1,2,3,4$: Berger quotients, flat axis quotients, nontrivial circle bundles over flat three-tori, and constant tori. This resolves Medvedev's index conjecture throughout the parameter range of the catenoid family. The kernel consists precisely of the normal components of rotations that preserve the center of the ball and tilt the axis of the catenoid. As a consequence, the radius of the supporting ball increases strictly along the family. The proof combines a global boundary inequality obtained from a Wronskian, a positive Dirichlet eigenfunction supplied by the time component of the unit normal, and a Riccati comparison for the angular mode $k=2$. A ground-state identity then yields positivity of every angular mode $k\ge2$.

    👥 Tianci Luo, Yong Wei, Ruijia Zhang, Rong Zhou

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究 H^{n+1}（n≥3）中由曲率商 (E_k/E_ℓ)^{1/(k-ℓ)} 驱动、保持 quermassintegral 的曲率流。对 1≤ℓ<k 且 k+ℓ≤n，我们证明正截面曲率被保持。从具有正截面曲率的光滑闭严格凸超曲面出发，流长时间存在并光滑指数收敛到测地球面。作为应用，我们对边界具非负截面曲率的光滑有界凸区域，在 0≤ℓ<k≤n 且 k+ℓ≤n 时建立 W_k 与 W_ℓ 之间的尖锐 Alexandrov-Fenchel 不等式，等号成立当且仅当区域为测地球。结合已知情形，这些不等式给出 H6 中该类区域的全族 Alexandrov-Fenchel 不等式。我们还构造反例，说明 k+ℓ≤n 是这些流保持正截面曲率的最优范围。

    We study quermassintegral-preserving curvature flows in $\mathbb H^{n+1}$, $n\geq3$, driven by the curvature quotient $(E_k/E_\ell)^{1/(k-\ell)}$. For $1\leq\ell<k$ with $k+\ell\leq n$, we prove that positive sectional curvature is preserved. Starting from a smooth closed strictly convex hypersurface with positive sectional curvature, the flow exists for all time and converges smoothly and exponentially to a geodesic sphere. As an application, we establish sharp Alexandrov--Fenchel inequalities between $W_k$ and $W_\ell$ for smooth bounded convex domains whose boundaries have nonnegative sectional curvature, whenever $0\leq\ell<k\leq n$ and $k+\ell\leq n$.     👥 Ting Li, Zhongwei Tang

    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study blow-up and compactness for the critical nonlinear Dirac equation \[ D_{g_0}\psi_i = H_i|\psi_i|^{\frac{2}{n-1}}\psi_i \qquad \text{on }\mathbb S^n,\quad n\ge 3, \] where $g_0$ is the round metric and $H_i\to H>0$ in $C^0(\mathbb S^n)$. The rescaled solutions converge to a ground-state Dirac bubble that carries the entire limiting critical mass. Under local uniform $C^1$ bounds on $H_i$, we use the Kelvin transform and a second rescaling to prove that $p$ is an isolated simple blow-up point. We also derive sharp pointwise and gradient estimates in cylindrical coordinates and identify the normalized Green limit. If the coefficients converge in $C^2$ near $p$, the Pohozaev identity yields $\operatorname{Hess}_{g_0}H(p)=0$. Consequently, if a positive $H\in C^2(\mathbb S^n)$ satisfies $\Delta_{g_0}H(p)<0$ at every global maximum, then solutions with critical mass bounded by a level slightly above the first threshold are uniformly bounded in $L^\infty(\mathbb S^n)$.     设 f: C^n → C 是次数为 n 的齐次多项式，在原点有孤立临界点。Calabi-Yau/Landau-Ginzburg 对应预言 Landau-Ginzburg 轨形 (C^n/Z_n, f) 的不变量与相伴 Calabi-Yau 超曲面 X_f = {[z_1:…:z_n] ∈ P^{n-1}: f(z_1,…,z_n)=0} 的不变量之间存在密切关系。本文对亏格一 B 模型建立该对应：从 C^n/Z_n 上的 f-扭曲拉普拉斯算子内蕴构造 Landau-Ginzburg 亏格一 B 模型不变量，并证明它与 X_f 的相应 BCOV 不变量相差一个仅依赖于维数的加性常数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $f:\mathbb C^n\to\mathbb C$ be a homogeneous polynomial of degree $n$ with an isolated critical point at the origin. The Calabi--Yau/Landau--Ginzburg correspondence predicts a close relation between invariants of the Landau--Ginzburg orbifold $(\mathbb C^n/\mathbb Z_n,f)$ and those of the associated Calabi--Yau hypersurface $ X_f=\{[z_1:\cdots:z_n]\in\mathbb P^{n-1}:f(z_1,\ldots,z_n)=0\}. $ In this paper, we establish this correspondence for the genus-one B-model by constructing the Landau--Ginzburg genus-one B-model invariant intrinsically from the $f$-twisted Laplacian on $\mathbb{C}^n/\mathbb{Z}_n$ and proving that it agrees with the corresponding BCOV invariant of $X_f$ up to an additive constant depending only on the dimension.

    Symplectic capacities and Weyl laws from Abelian Higgs min max theory
    👥 Taisuke Shibata
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文用阿贝尔希格斯能量的极小极大构造在四维定义一列辛容量。
    • 证明第一容量在球与柱上归一化，结合 J-全纯球族、平面涡旋与阿贝尔希格斯理论估计。
    • 利用 Seiberg-Witten 模空间得到高阶容量上界与柱上的线性公式。
    • 还证明了容量序列的欧氏 Weyl 律，开辟了辛容量构造的新分析途径。
    💡 用阿贝尔希格斯能量极小极大构造四维辛容量，证明球与柱的归一化及高维容量的 Weyl 律。
    📖 中文摘要

    对黎曼流形 (Y^n,g) 上的 Hermitian 线丛 E → Y，自对偶阿贝尔希格斯能量定义为 En^Y_{ε,g}(u,a) = (1/2π)∫_Y (|∇_a u|_g^2 + ε^2|F_a|_g^2 + (1-|u|^2)^2/(4ε^2)) dV_g，其中 u 是 E 的光滑截面，a 是酉联络，F_a 是其曲率。Pigati 与 Stern 将该能量作为 Allen-Cahn 能量的余维二类比来研究黎曼流形中的极小子流形。本文用该泛函通过极小极大构造在四维定义一列非递减的辛容量 {c_k^AH}_{k≥1}。特别地，我们证明 c_1^AH 在球与柱上被归一化。柱上的上界结合了 Gromov 的 J-全纯球族理论与 Taubes 的平面涡旋；球上的下界利用 Pigati-Stern 发展的阿贝尔希格斯理论性质，以及对球的双倍的光滑逼近的几何估计和低能量子水平的等变形变，从而无需 Seiberg-Witten 理论即得归一化。随后利用 CP^2 与 S^2×S^2 上的 Seiberg-Witten 模空间证明球的高阶容量上界以及公式 c_k^AH(Z^4(a), ω_0) = ka。我们还证明了容量序列与标准欧氏度量下极小极大序列的欧氏 Weyl 律，为此从扰动 Seiberg-Witten 方程解模空间构造 AH 扫掠族。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a Hermitian line bundle $E\to Y$ over a Riemannian manifold $(Y^n,g)$, the self-dual Abelian--Higgs energy is defined by $ \En^Y_{\eps,g}(u,a)=\frac1{2\pi}\int_Y \left(|\nabla_a u|_g^2+\eps^2|F_a|_g^2+ \frac{(1-|u|^2)^2}{4\eps^2}\right)dV_g, $ where $u$ is a smooth section of $E$, $a$ is a unitary connection, and $F_a$ is its curvature. Pigati and Stern studied the energy as a codimension-two analogue of the Allen--Cahn energy to study minimal submanifolds in a Riemannian manifold. In this paper, we use this functional to define a nondecreasing sequence of symplectic capacities $\{c_k^{\AH}\}_{k\ge1}$ in dimension four by a min--max construction. In particular, we prove that $c_1^{\AH}$ is normalized on the ball and the cylinder. For the upper bound on the cylinder, we combine Gromov's theory of families of $J$-holomorphic spheres with Taubes's planar vortex. For the lower bound on the ball, we use the properties of the Abelian--Higgs theory developed by Pigati--Stern, together with a geometric estimate on smooth approximations to the double of the ball and an equivariant deformation of low energy sublevels. These estimates give the normalization without Seiberg--Witten theory. We then use Seiberg--Witten moduli spaces on $\CP^2$ and $S^2\times S^2$ to prove an upper bound on the higher capacities of balls and the formula $c_k^{\AH}(Z^4(a),\omega_0)=ka$. We also prove Euclidean Weyl laws for the capacity sequence and for the min--max sequence with the standard Euclidean metric. For these results, we construct AH sweepouts from moduli spaces of the solutions to the perturbed Seiberg--Witten equations.

  44. [2610.34309] 超越 Brown 径向判别：可容许性、抛物性与一个双重 Poincaré 先验
    Beyond Brown's radial tests: admissibility, parabolicity, and a doubling-Poincar\'e prior that neither test decides
    👥 Oleg Roshka
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文把 Brown 的正态均值可容许性理论解读为加权欧氏空间上的势论。
    • 在双重测度与 Poincaré 不等式下证明球面判别已完备，几何判据只能补充不可容许情形。
    • 构造 R^3 上显式先验使 Brown 两判别沉默但估计量不可容许。
    • 在双重-Poincaré 类之外给出双侧例子，并用比较引理说明显式权可作先验边缘。
    💡 在 Brown 两个径向判别都失效的区域，用加权流形上的势论给出可容许与不可容许的新判据与显式先验。
    📖 中文摘要

    在二次损失下估计 R^n 中的正态均值时，Brown (1971) 证明广义 Bayes 估计量 δ_F 可容许当且仅当与先验 F 的边缘 f* 相联系的扩散是常返的，并给出两个径向判别：用于常返的球面平均判别和用于暂态的射线判别。他注意到两个判别都可能沉默。我们把他的理论解读为加权欧氏空间 (R^m, f* dx) 上的势论，并追问加权流形上现代常返性理论在两个判别都不适用的区域内能补充什么。三个结果。(A) 若 f* dx 是双重测度并支持尺度不变 Poincaré 不等式，则球面判别已经完备：常返性、Brown 球面判别与球体积判别等价，因此几何判据只能在不可容许一侧增加可判定情形。(B) 它们确实增加了一个。R^3 上一个显式先验，其边缘在二进尺度上沿间距为尺度平方根的圆格点很小，是双重测度且满足 Poincaré，使 Brown 两个判别都沉默，而由球体积判别是暂态的；其广义 Bayes 估计量不可容许。(C) 在双重-Poincaré 类之外该区域是双侧的：同样的圆每尺度放在一个曲面上给出可容许估计量，由沿非球面穷竭的流量界判定；而对数乘积先验给出不可容许估计量，由乘积热核判定。一个比较引理表明任何对数 Lipschitz 的正权在常数意义下都是某先验的边缘，因此可全程使用显式权。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For the estimation of a normal mean in $\mathbb{R}^m$ under quadratic loss, Brown (1971) proved that a generalized Bayes estimator $\delta_F$ is admissible if and only if a diffusion attached to the marginal $f^*$ of the prior $F$ is recurrent, and gave two radial tests: a sphere-average test for recurrence and a ray test for transience. He observed that both tests can be silent. We read his theory as the potential theory of the weighted Euclidean space $(\mathbb{R}^m,f^*\,dx)$ and ask what the modern theory of recurrence on weighted manifolds adds inside the zone where neither test applies. Three results. (A) If $f^*\,dx$ is doubling and supports a scale-invariant Poincar\'e inequality, the sphere test is already complete: recurrence, Brown's sphere test and the ball-volume test are equivalent, so geometric criteria can add decidable cases only on the inadmissible side. (B) They do add one. An explicit prior on $\mathbb{R}^3$ whose marginal is small along a lattice of circles at dyadic scales with spacing equal to the square root of the scale, is doubling and Poincar\'e, silences both of Brown's tests, and is transient by the ball-volume test; its generalized Bayes estimator is inadmissible. (C) Outside the doubling-Poincar\'e class the zone is two-sided: the same circles placed on one surface per scale give an admissible estimator, decided by a flux bound along a non-spherical exhaustion, and a logarithmic product prior gives an inadmissible one, decided by product heat kernels. A comparison lemma shows that any positive weight with Lipschitz logarithm is, up to constants, the marginal of a prior, so that explicit weights may be used throughout.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34309

    • 主要困难是同时控制收缩视界附近的线性化方程与非线性余项，用加权 Hölder 估计闭合压缩论证。

    💡 在带两个小质量黑洞的紧三维流形上构造正宇宙常数静态爱因斯坦方程的轴对称光滑解。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们构造在圆作用下不变、满足带正宇宙常数的静态爱因斯坦方程的光滑黎曼度量与标量势。底流形是挖去两个不相交开球的紧三维球。势（或时移函数）在内部为正、在边界为零，边界由三个非退化视界组成。两个内边界分支的面积由两个任意参数控制，即黑洞质量，取为足够小且相当。每个内视界半径与各自质量参数线性缩放，而参考中心间距按总质量的立方根缩放。这两个尺度以不同速率取足够小，而宇宙学长度保持固定。主要分析困难是在收缩视界附近、中心之间与外部区域同时控制线性化方程与非线性余项。加权 Hölder 估计量化逆的增长，并表明将 Kottler 环带与 de Sitter 空间拼接的近似误差足够小以闭合压缩论证。即我们求解带两个障碍张量的约化椭圆爱因斯坦方程，通过变动两个轴向位置消去其系数。投影引入的辅助向量场（Killing 场）的收缩 Bianchi 恒等式与能量估计使其消失。最后，对固定宇宙常数，一个边界分支的内禀度量足以在等距与势乘以正常数意义下确定内部单连通且边界梯度非零的静态解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We construct smooth Riemannian metrics and scalar potentials that are invariant under a circle action and satisfy the static Einstein equations with positive cosmological constant. The underlying compact three-manifold is a ball with two disjoint open balls removed. The potential (or lapse function) is positive in the interior and vanishes at the boundary, consisting of three non-degenerate horizons. The areas of the two inner boundary components are controlled by two arbitrary parameters, namely the masses of the black holes, which are taken to be sufficiently small and comparable. Each inner horizon radius scales linearly with the individual mass parameters, while the separation of their reference centers scales as the cube root of the total mass. These two scales are assumed to be sufficiently small at different rates, whereas the cosmological length remains fixed. The main analytical challenge is to control the linearized equation and the nonlinear remainder simultaneously near the shrinking horizons, between the centers and in the outer region. Weighted H\"older estimates quantify the growth of the inverse and show that the error of an approximation joining Kottler collars to de Sitter space is small enough to close a contraction argument. Namely, we solve a reduced elliptic Einstein equation that is endowed with two obstruction tensors; varying the two axial positions removes their coefficients. The contracted Bianchi identity and an energy estimate for an auxiliary vector field (Killing field) introduced by the projection to vanish. Finally, for a fixed cosmological constant, the intrinsic metric of one of the two boundary components is sufficient to determine a static solution with simply connected interior and non-vanishing boundary gradient, up to isometry and multiplication of the potential by a positive constant.

    💡 证明慢曲率速度各向异性 Gage 保面积流的有限时间爆破在长度有下界时为 II 型，并刻画爆破极限。
    📖 中文摘要

    本文研究浸入局部凸闭平面曲线在广义各向异性 Gage 保面积流下的奇点行为，速度形如 κ_σ^α，其中 0<α≤1，κ_σ 是相对于给定各向异性函数 σ 的各向异性曲率。我们证明若演化曲线的欧氏长度一致有正下界，则每个有限时间爆破都是 II 型。该结果对初始曲线与各向异性均无对称性假设。当初始有符号面积非零时，该长度下界自动满足，从而任何有限时间奇点必为 II 型。借助 Hamilton 重标度方法与微分 Harnack 估计，我们证明沿适当爆破子列，所选时刻的重标度曲线局部收敛到相应各向异性曲率流 V = κ_σ^α 的平移解轮廓。我们还用初始有符号面积与各向异性长度给出有限时间奇点形成的充分条件。此外，我们构造任意接近 Wulff 曲线的局部凸初始曲线，使其发生有限时间 II 型奇点。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we study the singularity behavior of immersed locally convex closed plane curves evolving under generalized anisotropic Gage area-preserving flows with slow curvature speeds of the form $\kappa_\sigma^\alpha$, where $0<\alpha\le 1$. Here $\kappa_\sigma$ denotes the anisotropic curvature associated with a prescribed anisotropy function $\sigma$. We prove that every finite-time blow-up is of Type-II provided that the Euclidean length of the evolving curve remains uniformly bounded away from zero. This result holds without any symmetry assumptions on either the initial curve or the anisotropy. When the initial signed area is nonzero, this length lower bound is automatically satisfied, and thus any finite-time singularity is necessarily of Type-II. By means of Hamilton's rescaling method combined with a differential Harnack estimate, we show that along a suitable blow-up subsequence, the rescaled curves at the selected times converge locally to the profile of a translating solution of the corresponding anisotropic curvature flow $V=\kappa_\sigma^\alpha$. We also establish sufficient conditions for finite-time singularity formation in terms of the initial signed area and anisotropic length. Moreover, we construct locally convex initial curves arbitrarily close to the Wulff curve for which a finite-time Type-II singularity occurs.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35064

  51. [2610.35431] 复射影区域上的对数凹性与基本间隙
    Log-Concavity and Fundamental Gaps on Complex Projective Domains
    👥 Gunhee Cho
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文在复射影区域上研究 Dirichlet Schrödinger 算子的定量对数凹性与基本间隙。
    • 在全纯截面曲率为四及边界曲率条件下证明 Hess(-log φ_1) > g 与显式谱间隙下界。
    • 方法扩展修正 Hessian 首次接触论证，保留 Hopf 项并用迹极小化归约梯度方向。
    • 这是 CP^n 区域在无对称性假设下的首批对数凹性估计，并给出直径与 Hessian 界。
    💡 在 CP^n 区域上证明定量对数凹性与谱间隙估计，无需对称性假设，并给出直径与 Hessian 界。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明复射影区域上 Dirichlet Schrödinger 算子的定量对数凹性与基本间隙估计。在全纯截面曲率为四时，在 Hess V ≥ 2|∇V|g 与 osc V ≤ 4n 条件下，边界条件 II ≥ √(44/5) g 在每个复维数下都足够。它给出 Hess(-log φ_1) > g 以及 λ_2 - λ_1 > max{n+2+√(n^2+5n+11), 2n+14/3}。主要困难是同时控制特殊的 Hopf 方向与维数随 n 增长的横向 Hessian 块。我们扩展修正 Hessian 首次接触论证，保留 Hopf 项并使用纵向与横向旋转检验。迹极小化将所有梯度方向归约为三个极端方向。一个按维数缩放的对数障碍函数随后用单一极限轮廓比较它们的接触代价。一个径向谱估计从与维数无关的边界曲率界给出所需的二次维数谱阈值。对 n≥3，据我们所知这是 CP^n 中区域在无对称性假设下的首批对数凹性估计。我们还在更大的无维数曲率界下证明尖锐直径界与线性于 n 的 Hessian 水平。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove quantitative log-concavity and fundamental-gap estimates for Dirichlet Schr\"odinger operators on complex projective domains. With holomorphic sectional curvature four, the boundary condition $\mathrm{II}\ge\sqrt{44/5}\,g$ suffices in every complex dimension under $\operatorname{Hess} V\ge2|\nabla V|g$ and $\operatorname{osc}V\le4n$. It yields $\operatorname{Hess}(-\log\phi_1)>g$ and $\lambda_2-\lambda_1>\max\{n+2+\sqrt{n^2+5n+11},\,2n+14/3\}$. The main difficulty is to control the distinguished Hopf direction together with a transverse Hessian block whose dimension grows with $n$. We extend modified-Hessian first-contact arguments by retaining the Hopf term and using longitudinal and transverse rotational tests. A trace minimization reduces all gradient orientations to three extreme directions. A dimension-scaled logarithmic barrier then compares their contact costs with a single limiting profile. A radial spectral estimate supplies the required quadratic-in-dimension spectral threshold from a dimension-independent boundary curvature bound. For $n\ge3$ these are, to our knowledge, the first log-concavity estimates for domains in $\mathbb{CP}^n$ without symmetry assumptions. We also prove a sharp diameter bound and Hessian levels linear in $n$ under larger dimension-free curvature bounds.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35431

    📖 中文摘要

    弦耦合到局部描述未必能拼成单一整体形式的二形式规范势。主 2-丛为这种粘合提供几何描述。然而，我们无需 2-丛形式即可恢复曲面振幅与量子配分函数以及局部经典场动力学。对于整体相位、拓扑与指标不变量以及扭曲层模空间，已有等价性通过上链、丛、算子或模给出替代描述，并带有所需的整体相容数据。对带上同调作用的三维有限规范理论，傅里叶变换将配分函数表示为普通有限群规范理论配分函数之和。一个可逆局部替换将一类由有限维交叉模表示的 2-丛联络的不变量经典作用量约化为普通规范与张量动力学。这些有限与局部约化显式消去场变量。一个四维二次例子即使普通规范理论约化失败也可直接做上链求值。因此这些结果并不需要额外的 2-丛表述。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A string couples to a two-form gauge potential whose local descriptions need not fit together as a single global form. Principal 2-bundles provide a geometric description of this gluing. However, we recover surface amplitudes and quantum partition functions, as well as local classical field dynamics, without the 2-bundle formalism. For global phases, topological and index invariants, and twisted-sheaf moduli, established equivalences give alternative descriptions through cocycles, bundles, operators, or modules, with the required global compatibility data. For three-dimensional finite gauge theories with cohomological actions, Fourier transformation expresses the partition function as a sum of partition functions of ordinary finite-group gauge theories. An invertible local substitution reduces invariant classical actions for a class of 2-bundle connections represented by finite-dimensional crossed modules to ordinary gauge and tensor dynamics. These finite and local reductions explicitly eliminate field variables. A four-dimensional quadratic example instead admits a direct cochain evaluation even when an ordinary gauge-theory reduction fails. These results therefore do not require the additional 2-bundle presentation.

    Characteristic gluing to extremal Kerr horizons and the third law of black hole thermodynamics in vacuum
    👥 Stefan Czimek
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文证明爱因斯坦真空方程的非微扰非轴对称特征粘合定理。
    • 可将 Kerr 外通信区域中任一 Boyer-Lindquist 球面粘合到足够大质量极端 Kerr 视界球面，匹配完整球面数据。
    • 方法用旋转剪切脉冲提升角动量，再沿长颈部指数衰减入射数据，并反演线性化问题。
    • 由此构造真空中第三定律的首个严格反例，证明 Kehle-Unger 的两个猜想。
    💡 证明非轴对称特征粘合定理，将 Kerr 球面粘到极端 Kerr 视界，构造真空中第三定律的首个反例。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明爱因斯坦真空方程的一个非微扰、非轴对称特征粘合定理：Kerr 黑洞外通信区域中的每个 Boyer-Lindquist 球面，特别是 Schwarzschild 外部的每个对称球面，都可沿单一出射类光超曲面粘合到足够大质量的极端 Kerr 黑洞的视界球面。粘合在两端精确，并匹配完整球面数据，包括横向曲率。这证明了 Kehle 与 Unger 在极端情形的猜想。我们首先通过非轴对称引力辐射的旋转剪切脉冲将角动量提升到极端值，然后沿长非膨胀颈部指数衰减入射数据。在退化目标视界处，我们在所有角频率上反演线性化问题，利用四阶角算子分解为角 Teukolsky-Starobinsky 乘积。作为应用，我们构造单端渐近平坦真空初始数据，其极大发展包含精确 Schwarzschild 视界，并在较晚超前时刻包含精确极端 Kerr 事件视界。这是真空中黑洞热力学第三定律的首个严格反例，并证明了 Kehle 与 Unger 的第二个猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove a non-perturbative, non-axisymmetric characteristic gluing theorem for the Einstein vacuum equations: every Boyer-Lindquist sphere in the domain of outer communication of a Kerr black hole, in particular every symmetry sphere of a Schwarzschild exterior, can be glued along a single outgoing null hypersurface to a horizon sphere of an extremal Kerr black hole of sufficiently large mass. The gluing is exact at both ends and matches the full sphere data, including the transversal curvature. This proves a conjecture of Kehle and Unger in the extremal case. We first raise the angular momentum to the extremal value by rotating shear pulses of non-axisymmetric gravitational radiation, and then damp the incoming data exponentially along a long non-expanding neck. At the degenerate target horizon we invert the linearised problem at all angular frequencies, using a factorisation of a fourth-order angular operator into an angular Teukolsky-Starobinsky product. As an application, we construct one-ended asymptotically flat vacuum initial data whose maximal development contains an exactly Schwarzschild apparent horizon and, at a later advanced time, an exactly extremal Kerr event horizon. This is the first rigorous counterexample to the third law of black hole thermodynamics in vacuum, and proves a second conjecture of Kehle and Unger.

    💡 证明高维过阻尼朗之万扩散在临界时间出现截断现象，并联系谱刚性。
    📖 中文摘要

    考虑欧氏空间中的过阻尼朗之万扩散，其曲率等于谱隙。这包括Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程以及具有凸相互作用的非高斯和非乘积推广，如随机矩阵理论中的Dyson过程。我们证明在高维中，在临界时间等于维数对数除以两倍谱隙时，会出现截断现象或突然收敛到平衡态。该截断对Wasserstein距离、全变差、相对熵和Fisher信息均成立。一个关键观察是与谱刚性的关系，这与高斯因子的存在有关。新颖之处在于大量使用泛函不等式，甚至用于短时正则化，并归约到Wasserstein距离。证明简短且概念清晰。由于乘积条件满足，对所有有限p成立Lp截断。此外，我们讨论了到黎曼流形的自然推广、与热核短时对数梯度估计的联系，并询问扰动下的稳定性。最后，在刚性之外但仍针对扩散，围绕非负曲率扩散乘积条件的最新进展进行讨论，引入新的曲率乘积条件。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We consider overdamped Langevin diffusions in Euclidean space, with curvature equal to the spectral gap. This includes the Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process as well as non-Gaussian and non-product extensions with convex interaction, such as the Dyson process from random matrix theory. We show that a cutoff phenomenon or abrupt convergence to equilibrium occurs in high dimension, at a critical time equal to the logarithm of the dimension divided by twice the spectral gap. This cutoff holds for Wasserstein distance, total variation, relative entropy, and Fisher information. A key observation is a relation to a spectral rigidity, linked to the presence of a Gaussian factor. A novelty is the extensive usage of functional inequalities, even for short-time regularization, and the reduction to Wasserstein. The proofs are short and conceptual. Since the product condition is satisfied, an Lp cutoff holds for all finite p. We moreover discuss a natural extension to Riemannian manifolds, a link with logarithmic gradient estimates in short-time for the heat kernel, and ask about stability by perturbation. Finally, beyond rigidity but still for diffusions, a discussion around the recent progress on the product condition for non-negatively curved diffusions leads us to introduce a new curvature product condition.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15969

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究和比较两类主要紧Shimura曲面上的复曲线行为：Picard模曲面和Hilbert模曲面的紧变体。它们是复双曲球的商，对应于具有特定自同态类型的阿贝尔簇的模问题。我们证明，如果固定亏格界，则当定义判别式足够大时，Picard模曲面紧变体上不存在低亏格曲线，而Hilbert模曲面紧变体上低亏格曲线可能持续存在，但仅当它们是特殊曲线，即参数化具有额外自同态的曲线。这些结果是阿贝尔簇自同态类型一致有界性的复函数域模拟的特殊情形。证明需要理解特殊子簇的排斥性，并依赖于双曲几何中的体积估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We investigate and compare the behaviors of complex curves on two major types of compact Shimura surfaces: compact variants of Picard and Hilbert modular surfaces. They are quotients of complex hyperbolic balls corresponding to moduli problems for abelian varieties with certain endomorphism types. We prove that if we fix a genus bound, then when the defining discriminant is large enough, low genus curves on the compact variant of Picard modular surfaces do not exist, while low genus curves may persist on the compact variant of Hilbert modular surfaces only if they are special curves, which parametrize those with extra endomorphisms. These results are special cases of a complex function field analogue of uniform boundedness on endomorphism types of abelian varieties. The proof requires understanding the repulsion of special subvarieties and relies on volume estimates in hyperbolic geometry.

The proof combines convex barriers at infinity, distance comparison for $C^{1,1}$ hypersurfaces and a spectral maximum principle. For surfaces, no lower curvature bound is needed.

    🌐 English Abstract

    This paper provides an isomorphism between the space of twisted gauge fields on a principal bundle $\mathcal{P}(M,H)$ and the space of standard gauge fields on a different principal bundle $\mathcal{Q}(M,G)$ associated to $\mathcal{P}$. This isomorphism extends to local fields on the base manifold, which enables the use of local twisted fields in standard gauge theories (e.g. Yang-Mills-like theories). This allows one to deal with two symmetry groups, coming from $\mathcal{P}$ and $\mathcal{Q}$, respectively. The construction makes use of a larger principal bundle $\mathcal{S}$ which has $\mathcal{P}$ and $\mathcal{Q}$ as quotient bundles. $\mathcal{S}$ is a Hilsum-Skandalis bibundle between the action groupoids $H\ltimes\mathcal{P}$ and $G\ltimes\mathcal{Q}$. The gauge structure on $\mathcal{S}$ encodes both standard and twisted gauge structures on $\mathcal{P}$. In addition, the isomorphism classes of bundles $\mathcal{S}$ up to an isomorphism covering the identity map on $\mathcal{P}$ are in 1:1 correspondence with the equivalence classes of cocycles (up to a coboundary). This paper also provides a new interpretation of (full) dressing fields as dynamic (or active) sections of a principal bundle.

    我们研究固定架构ReLU神经网络实现函数空间的几何与闭包。首先建立关于连续分段线性函数极限的一般结果：函数仿射的凸集最小数目在逐点收敛下是下半连续的。证明利用与超图着色的联系和紧性原理，并给出连续极限多面体复杂度的定量界。然后刻画具有向量值输出的浅层ReLU网络可实现的函数，推广已知的标量值刻画。利用此和极限超平面配置的几何，我们证明固定宽度浅层标量ReLU实现空间在整个输入空间的加权上确界范数下是闭的。相反，我们构造架构(2,3,2|1)的显式网络序列，其极限不能由该架构实现。非闭性在加权上确界范数和内部非空紧集上的一致收敛下均成立，否定了Petersen、Raslan和Voigtlaender的猜想。最后，我们证明固定标量输出ReLU架构的非闭性必然涉及爆破残余，其中发散输出系数放大隐藏特征间新出现的线性依赖。这些结果为研究ReLU实现空间的边界和退化提供几何和泛函分析工具。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the geometry and closure of spaces of functions realized by ReLU neural networks of fixed architecture. We first establish a general result about limits of continuous piecewise-linear (CPWL) functions: the minimum number of convex sets on which a function is affine is lower semicontinuous under pointwise convergence. The proof uses a connection to hypergraph coloring and a compactness principle, and yields quantitative bounds on the polyhedral complexity of continuous limits. We then characterize the functions realizable by shallow ReLU networks with vector-valued outputs, extending a known scalar-valued characterization. Using this and the geometry of limiting hyperplane arrangements, we prove that fixed-width shallow scalar ReLU realization spaces are closed in the weighted supremum norm on the whole input space. In contrast, we construct an explicit sequence of networks with architecture $(2,3,2\mid1)$ whose limit is not realizable by that architecture. Non-closedness holds both globally in weighted supremum norm and under uniform convergence on compact sets with nonempty interior, disproving a conjecture of Petersen, Raslan, and Voigtlaender. Finally, we show that non-closedness for a fixed scalar-output ReLU architecture necessarily involves a \emph{blow-up residue}, in which diverging output coefficients amplify an emerging linear dependence among hidden features. Together, these results provide geometric and functional-analytic tools for studying the boundaries and degenerations of ReLU realization spaces.

    👥 Linh Truong
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 证明Upsilon斜率条件推出b(K)>n，推广已有结果。
    • 证明特定Alexander分级上纽结Floer同调非零。
    • 构造b(K)任意大的纤维缆纽结，并讨论单调非转向情形。
    💡 证明Upsilon斜率条件推出b(K)>n，并给出纤维纽结Floer同调非消没等应用。
    📖 中文摘要

    对于有理同调球中的零同调纤维纽结K，Baldwin和Vela-Vick定义了Ozsváth-Szabó接触不变量的加细b(K)。我们证明若Ozsváth-Stipsicz-Szabó和谐不变量Upsilon在某个t∈[0,2/(n+1))满足斜率Upsilon'_K(t)=-g(K)（g(K)为K的亏格），则b(K)>n。这推广了He、Hubbard和作者关于Upsilon不变量与右转单调的结果。我们还证明纤维纽结在g(K)-b(K) Alexander分级上的纽结Floer同调非零。作为应用，给出许多纤维缆纽结族其b(K)任意大。最后证明若纤维纽结的单调既非右转也非左转，则纽结Floer同调的第三高项非零。

    🌐 English Abstract

We prove that if the Ozsv\'ath-Stipsicz-Szab\'o concordance invariant Upsilon has slope $\Upsilon'_K(t)=-g(K)$ for some $t\in[0,\frac{2}{n+1})$, where $g(K)$ is the genus of $K$, then $b(K)>n$. This extends a result of He, Hubbard, and the author on the Upsilon invariant and right-veering monodromies. We also prove that the knot Floer homology of a fibered knot in the $g(K)-b(K)$ Alexander grading is nonvanishing. As an application, we give many families of fibered cable knots whose $b(K)$ invariant grows arbitrarily large. Finally, we prove that if a monodromy of a fibered knot is neither right-veering nor left-veering, then the third-to-top term of knot Floer homology is nonvanishing.

The spaces we are interested in are spaces of complex monic polynomials of degree $n$ whose critical values lie in $\mathbb{C} \setminus \{1, e^{\frac{2\pi i}{k}}, \dots, e^{\frac{2\pi i (k-1)}{k}}\}$.     扭曲环面纽结 T(p,q;r,s) 是通过在平坦环面图 T(p,q) 上沿与 r 条相邻股相交的圆盘插入 s 个全扭曲得到的。我们之前的工作确定了当 1<r≤max{|p|,|q|} 时的 Alexander 多项式。本文中，我们通过将纽结放置在标准亏格二 Heegaard 曲面上并应用 Fox 微积分，计算了互补范围 max{|p|,|q|}<r≤|p|+|q| 的情形。然后我们将两个计算结合成一个统一的 Alexander 多项式公式，适用于 1<r≤|p|+|q|。在互补范围内，我们证明了单项性并给出了宽度的显式公式。这确定了非负扭曲时的 Seifert 亏格，并给出了负扭曲时的亏格下界。我们还展示了互补范围内无限的双参数非 L-空间纽结族。

    🌐 English Abstract

Our previous work determined its Alexander polynomial for 1<r<=max{|p|,|q|}. In this paper, we compute it in the complementary range max{|p|,|q|}<r<=|p|+|q| by placing the knot on a standard genus-two Heegaard surface and applying Fox calculus. We then combine the two calculations into a unified Alexander-polynomial formula valid for 1<r<=|p|+|q|. In the complementary range, we prove monicity and give an explicit formula for the breadth.     👥 Wenbiao Liu

    💡 定义了定向链环的提升亏格，证明其在 t3 和 t6 移动下不变，并应用于区分纽结等价类。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们通过将 Fox 3-着色提升到九元 Alexander quandle 的着色问题来研究定向 t3-和 t6-移动。我们为定向链环定义了一个非负整数提升亏格 δ，并证明它在这些移动下不变。对于纽结，该亏格等于由 Seifert 矩阵得到的交错双线性形式的秩，因此是偶数。我们还证明它在连通和下可加。设 K_s 是 s≥1 个 9_46 副本的连通和。则 δ(K_s)=2s，因此这些纽结在定向移动下代表无限多个不同的等价类，且没有一个等价于平凡链环。忘记定向后，每个 K_s 可通过 3s 个普通 3-移动变换为具有 2s+1 个分支的平凡链环。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study oriented $t_3$- and $\bar t_6$-moves through the problem of lifting Fox $3$-colorings to colorings by a nine-element Alexander quandle. We define a nonnegative integer lifting defect $\delta$ for oriented links and prove that it is invariant under these moves. For knots, the defect equals the rank of an alternating bilinear form obtained from a Seifert matrix, so it is even. We also prove that it is additive under connected sum. Let $K_s$ be the connected sum of $s\geq 1$ copies of $9_{46}$. Then $\delta(K_s)=2s$, so these knots represent infinitely many distinct equivalence classes under the oriented moves, and none is equivalent to an unlink. After forgetting orientation, each $K_s$ can be transformed into an unlink with $2s+1$ components by $3s$ ordinary $3$-moves.

    本文研究高度 4 纽结，它推广了 Ozsváth-Szabó、Baldwin-Sivek 和 Binns 研究的 L-空间纽结和几乎 L-空间纽结。若对任意整数 n≥2g(K)-1，有 rank HF^(S^3_n(K))-n=m，则称纽结 K⊂S^3 为高度 m 纽结，其中 HF^ 是 Ozsváth-Szabó 引入的 Heegaard Floer 同调的帽子版本。特别地，高度 0 和高度 2 纽结分别恰好是 L-空间纽结和几乎 L-空间纽结。我们证明高度 4 纽结 K 的纽结 Floer 复形 CFK^∞(S^3,K) 具有水平与垂直简化的基——简称 hv-基。此外，我们证明在此 hv-基下，水平和垂直微分分为六种情形，推广了 L-空间纽结和几乎 L-空间纽结的结果。K 的纽结 Floer 复形的 hv-基可通过显式算法确定 K 的补的 Hanselman-Rasmussen-Watson 浸入曲线。因此，我们的结果有助于进一步探索两个高度 4 纽结补的粘合的 HF^ 计算。

    🌐 English Abstract

    In this paper, we study height 4 knots, which generalize L-space knots and almost L-space knots studied by Ozsv\'ath--Szab\'o, Baldwin--Sivek, and Binns. A knot $K\subset S^3$ is called a height $m$ knot if $\operatorname{rank} \widehat{HF}(S^3_n(K))-n=m$ for all integers $n\geq 2g(K)-1$, where $\widehat{HF}$ is the hat version of Heegaard Floer homology introduced by Ozsv\'ath--Szab\'o. In particular, height 0 and height 2 knots are exactly L-space knots and almost L-space knots, respectively. We prove that the knot Floer complex $CFK^\infty(S^3,K)$ of a height 4 knot $K$ admits a basis that is horizontally and vertically simplified--an hv-basis for short. Moreover, we show that under such an hv-basis, the horizontal and vertical differentials fall into six cases, generalizing the results for L-space knots and almost L-space knots. The hv-basis of the knot Floer complex of $K$ can determine, via an explicit algorithm, the immersed curve of Hanselman-Rasmussen-Watson for the knot complement of $K$. Consequently, our results facilitate further exploration of computations of $\widehat{HF}$ for the gluing of two height 4 knot complements.

  5. [2610.33384] 正交型长根几何的绝对通用射影嵌入
    Absolutely universal projective embeddings of long-root geometries of orthogonal type
    👥 Antonio Pasini
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究正交型长根几何的通用射影嵌入。
    • 证明通用嵌入位于 K⊕L 中，其中 K 的对偶为导子空间 Der(F)。
    • 得出伴随嵌入通用当且仅当 Der(F) 平凡。
    • 这刻画了无限域上通用嵌入与伴随嵌入的差异。
    💡 证明了正交型长根几何的通用射影嵌入位于 K⊕L 中，并给出伴随嵌入通用的充要条件。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 G 是类型 B_n（n≥3）或 D_n（n≥4）的拆分 Chevalley 群，L 是其 Lie 代数，Γ 是其长根几何，即 Γ 是与 G 关联的极空间的线 Grassmann 流形。众所周知，L 承载 Γ 的一个射影嵌入，称为伴随嵌入，它将 Γ 实现为 L 的底层向量空间的射影几何中的射影簇。也知道 Γ 允许绝对通用射影嵌入，使得 Γ 的所有射影嵌入都可从中通过投影得到。当 G 定义在有限域 F 上时，Γ 的通用嵌入与伴随嵌入相同。当 F 无限时，这不一定成立。事实上，如我们在本文中将证明的，Γ 的通用射影嵌入位于直和 K⊕L 中，其中 K 是 F-向量空间，其对偶同构于 F 的导子空间 Der(F)，通常非平凡。因此，Γ 的伴随嵌入是通用的当且仅当 Der(F) 平凡，即 F 是正特征完美域或其最小子域的代数扩张。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $G$ be a split Chevalley group of type $B_n$ ($n \geq 3$) or $D_n$ ($n \geq 4$), let $L$ be its Lie algebra and $\Gamma$ its long-root geometry, namely $\Gamma$ is the line-grassmannian of the polar space associated to $G$. It is well known that $L$ hosts a projective embedding of $\Gamma$, called the adjoint embedding, which realizes $\Gamma$ as a projective variety in the projective geometry of the underlying vector space of $L$. It is also known that $\Gamma$ admits the absolutely universal projective embedding, which is such that all projective embeddings of $\Gamma$ can be obtained from it as projections. When the field $F$ over which $G$ is defined is finite, the universal embedding of $\Gamma$ is just the same as the adjoint embedding. The same is not necessarily true when $F$ is infinite. Indeed, as we shall prove in this paper, the universal projective embedding of $\Gamma$ lives in a direct sum $K\oplus L$ for an $F$-vector space $K$, the dual of which is isomorphic to the derivation space $\mathrm{Der}(F)$ of $F$, which in general is non-trivial. Accordingly, the adjoint embedding of $\Gamma$ is universal if and only if $\mathrm{Der}(F)$ is trivial, namely $F$ is either perfect of positive characteristic or algebraic over its minimum subfield.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33384

    📖 中文摘要

    对称 Cayley 图的分类引起了广泛关注。对于有限交换群和非交换单群已获得结果。然而，对于有限非交换特征单群，问题仍未解决，尽管关于小价数正规性的一些结果已知。在本文中，我们确定了 G≅T^k（T 为有限非交换单群，k≥2）的连通 2-弧传递 Cayley 图 Γ 的自同构群 X 的可能结构。在拟本原情形，我们证明 G⊴X、Γ 是完全图，或 X 是参数受限的积作用型。对于非拟本原情形，我们确定了 Γ 的拟本原正规商的可能结构。

    The classification of symmetric Cayley graphs has attracted considerable attention. Results have been obtained for finite abelian groups and nonabelian simple groups. In this paper, we determine the possible structures of automorphism groups $X$ of connected 2-arc-transitive Cayley graphs $\Gamma$ of $G\cong T^k$, where $T$ is a finite nonabelian simple group and $k\geqslant2$. In the quasiprimitive case, we prove that $G\unlhd X$, $\Gamma$ is complete, or $X$ is of product action type with restricted parameters. For the non-quasiprimitive case, we determine the possible structures of the quasiprimitive normal quotients of $\Gamma$.

    📖 中文摘要

    本文证明所有具有非单模 Bass-Serre 树的非可均 Baumslag-Solitar 群，即 |p|,|q|≠1 且 |p|≠|q| 的 BS(p,q)，彼此测度等价，从而完成 Baumslag-Solitar 群的测度等价分类：每个 BS(p,q) 根据其是可均的（|p|=1 或 |q|=1）、虚拟同构于 F_n×Z（|p|=|q|≠1）、还是非可均且具有非单模 Bass-Serre 树（|p|,|q|≠1 且 |p|≠|q|）属于三个测度等价类之一。更一般地，我们得到秩 1 广义 Baumslag-Solitar 群的类似分类。我们的证明还建立了所有树化 lcsc 群到恰好六个测度等价类的分类。我们的新贡献是：所有非单模非可均树化 lcsc 群测度等价。在此过程中，我们获得了关于 III 型等价关系和图测度的若干独立结果。例如，我们证明每个遍历 III_0 型图测度允许一个遍历超有限子图，使得 Krieger 流的自然因子映射是同构。

    🌐 English Abstract

Our proof also establishes a classification of all treeable lcsc groups into exactly six measure equivalence classes. Our new contribution which completes this taxonomy is: all nonunimodular nonamenable treeable lcsc groups are measure equivalent. Along the way we obtain several results about type $\mathrm{III}$ equivalence relations and graphings of independent interest. For example, we show that every ergodic type $\mathrm{III}_0$ graphing admits an ergodic hyperfinite subgraph for which the natural factor map of Krieger flows is an isomorphism.

A Kazhdan projection estimate shows that, in any $G$-action weakly contained in finite actions, every $H$-invariant measurable subset is also $N$-invariant.     🌐 English Abstract

The results are for all activations with or without a bias term, and shed insight into the structure of the infinite width DNTK. Then we prove that the integral operators over the unit sphere induced by operators from the same group orbit share the spectrum, and their reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces differ only by an orthogonal transformation, and that the operator fixed DNTK exhibits zonal symmetry on the unit sphere for symmetric operators.   18. [2610.34695] 圈积约化 C*-代数的局部自反性与精确性
    Local Reflexivity and Exactness for Reduced $C^*$-Algebras of Wreath Products
    👥 Pingchuan Dong, Zhe Dong
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究圈积约化 C*-代数的局部自反性。
    • 作者证明非精确群圈积的约化 C*-代数非局部自反，给出首批例子。
    • 并证明当 K 精确时局部自反性等价于 H 的精确性。
    • 部分回答了 Kirchberg 关于局部自反蕴含精确性的问题。
    • 应用构造了 Haagerup 性质群等反例。
    💡 证明非精确群圈积的约化 C*-代数非局部自反，首次给出此类例子，并部分回答 Kirchberg 问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明，对于每个可数非精确离散群 H 和每个非平凡可数离散群 K，约化群 C*-代数 C_r*(K ≀ H) 不是局部自反的。这提供了非局部自反约化群 C*-代数的首批已知例子，解决了一个长期存在的存在性问题。此外，对于每个非平凡可数精确离散群 K，我们建立 C_r*(K ≀ H) 的局部自反性等价于 H 的精确性，从而等价于 C_r*(K ≀ H) 的精确性。这为 Kirchberg 关于局部自反性是否蕴含 C*-代数精确性的问题提供了部分肯定回答。作为应用，我们构造了具有 Haagerup 性质的有限生成群，以及有限生成剩余有限群，其约化群 C*-代数不是局部自反的。特别地，Haagerup 性质和粗嵌入到 Hilbert 空间都不能保证相关约化群 C*-代数的局部自反性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove that, for every countable non-exact discrete group $H$ and every nontrivial countable discrete group $K$, the reduced group $C^*$-algebra $C_r^*(K\wr H)$ is not locally reflexive. These provide the first known examples of non-locally reflexive reduced group $C^*$-algebras, settling a longstanding existence question. Moreover, for every nontrivial countable exact discrete group $K$, we establish that local reflexivity of $C_r^*(K\wr H)$ is equivalent to exactness of $H$, and hence to exactness of $C_r^*(K\wr H)$. This gives a partial affirmative answer to Kirchberg's question of whether local reflexivity implies exactness for $C^*$-algebras. As applications, we construct finitely generated groups with the Haagerup property, as well as finitely generated residually finite groups, whose reduced group $C^*$-algebras are not locally reflexive. In particular, neither the Haagerup property nor coarse embeddability into Hilbert space guarantees local reflexivity of the associated reduced group $C^*$-algebra.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34695
  19. [2610.34902] 对称群及相关变换半群上变换诱导的交叉功
    Cross work induced by transformations on the symmetric group and related transformation semigroups
    👥 Nsibiet E. Udo, Victor A. Owai, Moses O. Francis
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文在对称群等四个变换半群上引入交叉功统计量。
    • 该统计量基于相邻位置及其像的局部距离交互定义。
    • 作者建立逆不变性并刻画保持该统计量的共轭。
    • 推导对称群上总交叉功与平均交叉功的闭公式。
    • 并确定极值，证明最大值随 n 二次增长。
    💡 在对称群等四个半群上引入交叉功统计量，建立不变性并计算总平均及极值。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们在部分变换半群 PT_n 的四个重要子半群上引入一个新的置换统计量，称为交叉功：对称群 S_n、奇异变换半群 Sing_n、对称逆半群 I_n 以及部分保序单射变换半群 POI_n。交叉功通过相邻位置及其像之间的局部距离交互定义。我们建立了基本不变性质，包括在自然对称条件下的逆不变性以及保持该统计量的共轭刻画。使用初等组合方法，我们推导了 S_n 上总交叉功和平均交叉功的闭公式。我们还确定了交叉功的精确最小值和最大值，表明最大值随 n 二次增长。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We introduce a new permutation statistic, called the \emph{cross work}, on four important subsemigroups of the partial transformation semigroup $\mathcal{PT}_n$: the symmetric group $\mathcal{S}_n$, the semigroup of singular transformations $\mathrm{Sing}_n$, the symmetric inverse semigroup $\mathcal{I}_n$, and the semigroup $\mathcal{POI}_n$ of partial order-preserving injective transformations. The cross work is defined via local distance interactions between adjacent positions and their images. Fundamental invariance properties are established, including inversion invariance under a natural symmetry condition and a characterization of conjugations preserving the statistic. Using elementary combinatorial methods, we derive closed formulas for the total and average cross work on $\mathcal{S}_n$. We also determine the exact minimum and maximum values of the cross work, showing that the maximal value grows quadratically with $n$.

  3. [2610.33975] 从十二面体生长的毕达哥拉斯树
    A Pythagoras Tree Grown from a Dodecahedron
    👥 Miguel Esteban Pérez Ibarra
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究由正十二面体构建的毕达哥拉斯树。
    • 作者从原始场景恢复参数并表述为迭代函数系统。
    • 证明树无自交，并确定缩放与扭转的毕达哥拉斯恒等式。
    • 证明冠的分形 Hausdorff 维数恰为 2。
    • 最后讨论全表面生长的奇偶性限制。
    💡 从十二面体构造毕达哥拉斯树，恢复参数并证明无自交，确定分形维数为 2。
    📖 中文摘要

    A. E. Bosman（1942）的毕达哥拉斯树用平面正方形绘制，已知的三维版本用立方体。本文记录了一棵用正十二面体构建的树：每个十二面体在单个五边形「冠」面的每条边上携带五个较小的副本，缩放因子 g ≈ 0.4346 并绕边扭转 45°。该构造最初仅以一对渲染图像和两个未文档化的 C++ 程序存在。我们从原始 Blender 场景中恢复其精确参数，将其重新表述为带凝聚的迭代函数系统，并用简短的计算机辅助论证证明它从不自交：两个十二面体仅当一个是另一个的父代时才重叠。十二面体六个面轴的毕达哥拉斯恒等式唯一确定了缩放 1/√5 和扭转 arccos(1/√5)。在这些值下，每个子代平贴于父代的一个面，五个子代的总表面积恰好等于父代，树的冠是 Hausdorff 维数恰为 2 的分形。最后我们问树能否同时在所有十二个面上生长。奇偶性论证表明在艺术品的比例下不能，但更小的缩放因子允许经过验证的无冲突全表面树。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The Pythagoras tree of A. E. Bosman (1942) is drawn with squares in the plane and, in the three-dimensional versions we know of, with cubes. This article documents a tree built instead from regular dodecahedra: each dodecahedron carries five smaller copies, one on each edge of a single pentagonal "crown" face, scaled by $g \approx 0.4346$ and twisted by $45^\circ$ about the edge. The construction first existed only as a pair of rendered images and two undocumented C++ programs. We recover its exact parameters from the original Blender scene, reformulate it as an iterated function system with condensation, and prove with a short computer-assisted argument that it never collides with itself: two of its dodecahedra overlap only when one is the parent of the other. A Pythagorean identity for the six face axes of the dodecahedron singles out the scale $1/\sqrt{5}$ and the twist $\arccos(1/\sqrt{5})$. With these values each child rests flat against a face of its parent, the five children together have exactly the parent's surface area, and the crown of the tree is a fractal of Hausdorff dimension exactly 2. Finally we ask whether the tree can grow on all twelve faces at once. A parity argument shows that at the artwork's proportions it cannot, but smaller scale factors admit certified clash-free all-faces trees.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33975

    我们从高一维的对称 Mahler 定理导出任意维数的 Makai 不等式 vol_n(K)vol_n((K-K)°) ≥ (n+1)/n!，等号恰对单形成立。证明使用经典的双层构造、其极体积的恒等式以及 Rogers–Shephard 体积估计。然后我们记录 Mahler 和 Makai 不等式对极 successive minima、Santaló 点处的 successive minima、临界行列式、第一覆盖极小和非可分格排列的基本推论。我们还将 Alexandrov–Fenchel 与体积乘积不等式结合，得到配对混合体积的尖锐界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We derive Makai's inequality $\vol_n(K)\vol_n((K-K)^\circ)\ge(n+1)/n!$ in every dimension from the symmetric Mahler theorem in one higher dimension, with equality precisely for simplices. The proof uses a classical two-layer construction, an identity for its polar volume, and a Rogers--Shephard volume estimate. We then record basic consequences of the Mahler and Makai inequalities for polar successive minima, successive minima at the Santal\'o point, critical determinants, first covering minima, and non-separable lattice arrangements. We also combine Alexandrov--Fenchel with the volume-product inequalities to obtain sharp bounds for paired mixed volumes.

  5. [2610.34651] 用多面体逼近欧几里得球：高维中的惊人速率
    Approximating the Euclidean Ball by Polytopes: Surprising Rates in High Dimensions
    👥 Yegor Gorodzha, Steven Hoehner, Gil Kur
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究用多面体逼近欧几里得球的相对对称差误差。
    • 作者确定尖锐维数依赖为 Θ(d^{-1/2})，改进已有上下界。
    • 并建立非渐近界，比较球与超立方体的逼近顶点数。
    • 证明球不最大化最优相对逼近误差。
    • 表明 Macbeath 定理不能推广到任意位置多面体。
    💡 确定用多面体逼近欧几里得球的相对对称差误差的尖锐维数依赖为 Θ(d^{-1/2})。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们确定用给定顶点数的多面体在相对对称差度量下逼近欧几里得单位球 B_2^d ⊂ R^d 的渐近逼近误差的尖锐维数依赖。具体地，我们证明 lim_{N→∞} N^{2/(d-1)} inf_{P∈P_d(N)} vol_d(P△B_2^d)/vol_d(B_2^d) = Θ(d^{-1/2})，其中 P_d(N) 由至多 N 个顶点且无包含限制的多面体组成。等价地，经典 Laguerre–Delone 三角剖分数满足 ldel_{d-1} = Θ(d^{-1/2})。这改进了 Böröczky（2000）的下界 Ω(d^{-1}) 和 Ludwig、Schütt 与 Werner（2006）的上界 O(1)。相比之下，Kur（2020）证明用给定面数逼近的相应渐近系数具有阶 1。我们还建立非渐近界，表明对每个固定 ε∈(0,1)，球允许用至多 exp(C(ε)√d) 个顶点的 ε-逼近，而将超立方体逼近到相同精度至少需要 exp(c(ε)d) 个顶点。因此，球在相同顶点预算下并不在所有凸体中最大化最优相对逼近误差，即使相差绝对常数因子。因此，Macbeath（1951）关于球在内接逼近中极值性的定理不能推广到任意位置的多面体。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We determine the sharp dimensional dependence of the asymptotic approximation error for the Euclidean unit ball $B_2^d\subset\mathbb{R}^d$ by polytopes with a prescribed number of vertices in the relative symmetric difference metric. Specifically, we prove that $$\lim_{N\to\infty}N^{2/(d-1)}\inf_{P\in P_d(N)}\frac{\operatorname{vol}_d(P\triangle B_2^d)}{\operatorname{vol}_d(B_2^d)}=\Theta(d^{-1/2}),$$ where $P_d(N)$ consists of polytopes with at most $N$ vertices and no containment restriction. Equivalently, the classical Laguerre-Delone triangulation numbers satisfy $\operatorname{ldel}_{d-1}=\Theta(d^{-1/2})$. This improves the lower bound $\Omega(d^{-1})$ of B\"or\"oczky (2000) and the upper bound $O(1)$ of Ludwig, Sch\"utt and Werner (2006). In contrast, the corresponding asymptotic coefficient for approximation with a prescribed number of facets has order one, as shown by Kur (2020). We also establish non-asymptotic bounds showing that, for every fixed $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$, the ball admits an $\varepsilon$-approximation with at most $\exp(C(\varepsilon)\sqrt d)$ vertices, whereas approximating the hypercube to the same accuracy requires at least $\exp(c(\varepsilon)d)$ vertices. Consequently, the ball does not maximize the optimal relative approximation error among convex bodies, even up to an absolute multiplicative constant at the same vertex budget. Thus Macbeath's theorem (1951) on the extremality of the ball for inscribed approximation does not extend to polytopes in arbitrary position.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34651
  6. [2610.33216] 无对称性或一般位置假设的随机游走吸收概率
    Absorption probabilities of random walks without symmetry or general position
    👥 Artyom Bolotin, Dmitry Zaporozhets
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究随机游走原点不被凸包包含的概率。
    • 作者在无对称性、一般位置或矩假设下证明一个恒等式。
    • 该恒等式基于部分和与循环和凸包的确定性排序恒等式。
    • 推论包括 Sparre Andersen、Spitzer 等经典结果。
    • 并给出原点永不被吸收的概率公式。
    💡 在无对称性、一般位置或矩假设下，给出随机游走原点不被凸包包含的概率恒等式。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 S_1, S_2, … 是 R^d 中具有独立同分布增量的随机游走。我们证明，对每个增量分布和每个 n≥1，P[0∉conv(S_1,…,S_n)] = Σ_{m=1}^n (1/m!) Σ_{k_1,…,k_m≥1, k_1+…+k_m=n} (1/(k_1…k_m)) P[0∉conv(S^{(1)}_{k_1},…,S^{(m)}_{k_m})]，其中 S^{(1)}, S^{(2)}, … 是游走的独立副本。不需要对称性、一般位置或矩假设。结果基于一个确定性恒等式：对任意向量 x_1,…,x_n，使得部分和的凸包避开原点的排序数等于使得循环和的凸包避开原点的置换数。我们从先前工作中得到的赋值置换平均恒等式推导它。推论包括 Sparre Andersen 和 Spitzer 的恒等式、Kabluchko、Vysotsky 和 Zaporozhets 的无分布吸收概率、Petrov、Randon-Furling 和 Zaporozhets 找到的生成函数，以及原点永不被吸收的概率公式。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $S_1,S_2,\dots$ be a random walk in $\mathbb R^d$ with independent identically distributed increments. We show that, for every distribution of the increments and for every $n\ge1$, \[ \mathbb P\bigl[0\notin\mathrm{conv}(S_1,\dots,S_n)\bigr] = \sum_{m=1}^n\frac1{m!}\sum_{\substack{k_1,\dots,k_m\ge1\\ k_1+\dots+k_m=n}}\frac{1}{k_1\cdots k_m}\,\mathbb P\bigl[0\notin\mathrm{conv}(S^{(1)}_{k_1},\dots,S^{(m)}_{k_m})\bigr], \] where $S^{(1)},S^{(2)},\dots$ are independent copies of the walk. No symmetry, general position or moment assumptions are needed. The result rests on a deterministic identity: for arbitrary vectors $x_1,\dots,x_n$, the number of orderings for which the convex hull of the partial sums avoids the origin equals the number of permutations for which the convex hull of the cycle sums avoids it. We derive it from a permutation-averaging identity for valuations obtained in our earlier work. Consequences include the identities of Sparre Andersen and Spitzer, the distribution-free absorption probabilities of Kabluchko, Vysotsky and Zaporozhets, their generating function found by Petrov, Randon-Furling and Zaporozhets, and a formula for the probability that the origin is never absorbed.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33216

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究与满足迹正则性条件的固定非负局部可积 Borel 密度相关的 (p,q)-加权对偶曲率测度。这些测度自然出现在 Wulff 形的变分公式中。当 g 是逐点 α-齐次（α∈R）或 g 是径向非增连续时，我们建立迹正则性条件。对于这两类中的非平凡偶密度，我们在适当假设下证明相关 Minkowski 问题偶版本的存在性结果。当给定测度具有有限支撑时，在密度的严格正性假设和参数的额外限制下，我们还建立唯一性。在 Lebesgue 测度情况下，我们的曲率测度退化为 Lutwak、Yang 和 Zhang 引入的测度，我们的变分公式恢复他们相应的变分公式。对于高斯测度，我们的结果与 Feng、Hu 和 Xu 以及 Feng、Li 和 Xu 的结果相关。最后，我们研究新测度的仿射性质及相关的混合体积。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study (p,q)-weighted dual curvature measures associated with a fixed nonnegative locally integrable Borel density satisfying a trace-regularity condition. These measures arise naturally from a variational formula for Wulff shapes. We establish the trace-regularity condition when g is pointwise \alpha-homogeneous for some \alpha\in R, or when g is radially non-increasing and continuous. For nontrivial even densities in either class, we prove existence results for the even version of the associated Minkowski problem under suitable assumptions. We also establish uniqueness when the prescribed measure has finite support, under a strict positivity assumption on the density and additional restrictions on the parameters. In the case of Lebesgue measure, our curvature measures reduce to those introduced by Lutwak, Yang, and Zhang, and our variational formulas recover their corresponding variational formula. For Gaussian measure, our results are related to those of Feng, Hu, and Xu, and Feng, Li, and Xu. Finally, we investigate the affine properties of the new measures and the associated mixed volumes.

    👥 Cyril Cayron

    • 结果给出单位晶体学四元数的平面对几何表示及复合规则。
    • 意义在于统一晶体学四元数与 Hamilton 四元数，并赋予虚数几何解释。
    💡 将四元数乘法推广到非笛卡尔基，用度量张量与交叉张量构造交叉度量张量，并给出晶体学四元数的几何解释。
    📖 中文摘要

    两个笛卡尔四元数的乘积可写成基于 4x4 矩阵的二次型，该矩阵由 Hamilton 引入的虚数 i、j、k 构成。我们把这一矩阵推广到非笛卡尔基，并证明该矩阵由度量张量与交叉张量组成，称之为交叉度量张量。其符号为 ia、ib、ic，即基本晶体学四元数。我们确定了六种晶族的交叉度量张量。我们还证明，任何单位晶体学四元数都可由无穷多对定向平面几何表示，这些平面沿该四元数的向量分量相交，且其夹角等于旋转的半角。四元数的复合遵循直观的源-靶规则。基本四元数 ia、ib、ic 分别由沿轴 a、b、c 相交的垂直定向平面对表示。我们还引入了另外三个互补四元数，即平面对 ma、mb、mc。基本四元数满足 Hamilton 关于虚数平方的规则，互补四元数满足 Hamilton 关于双乘积与三乘积的规则。对于笛卡尔基，四元数 i、j、k 表现为晶体学四元数的特例，由立方体的垂直面对 mx、my、mz 构成。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The product of two Cartesian quaternions can be written as a quadratic form based on a 4x4 matrix made of the imaginary numbers i,j,k introduced by Hamilton. We generalized this matrix to non-Cartesian bases, and showed that the matrix is made of the metric and the cross tensors. We called it crossmetric tensor. Its symbols are ia,ib,ic; they are the elementary crystallographic quaternions. We determined the crossmetric tensors for the six crystal families. We also showed that any unit crystallographic quaternion can be geometrically represented by an infinity of pairs of oriented planes intersecting along the vectorial component of the quaternion such that the angle between them is the semiangle of the rotation. The composition of quaternions follows the intuitive source-target rule. The elementary quaternions ia,ib,ic are geometrically represented by pair of perpendicular and oriented planes intersecting along the axis a,b,c, respectively. Three other complementary quaternions were also introduced. They are the pairs of planes ma, mb, mc. The elementary quaternions follow Hamilton rules on the squares of imaginary numbers. The complementary quaternions follow Hamilton rules on the bi and tri-products. For Cartesian basis, the quaternions i,j,k appear as a specific case of crystallographic quaternions. They are formed by the pairs of perpendicular faces of the cube mx, my, mz.

    👥 Ziwei Hong
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究函数域上三次 L-函数一阶矩的渐近展开。
    • 作者对双 Dirichlet 级数的筛变量取平均，获得亚纯延拓。
    • 结合三次 Gauss 和留数，得到显式次主项 q^{5g/6} 及误差估计。
    • 结果揭示了周期系数非零，深化了函数域 L-函数矩的理解。
    💡 在 q 模 3 余 2 的函数域上，得到三次 Dirichlet L-函数一阶矩的显式次主项 q^{5g/6}。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 q 为固定奇素数幂且 q 模 3 余 2。我们研究 Fq(T) 上本原三次 Dirichlet L-函数按亏格排序的一阶矩。通过对 Hong 与 Fang 的双 Dirichlet 级数中的有理筛变量取平均，我们得到越过次极除子的亚纯延拓。结合 David、Florea 与 Lalin 计算的三次 Gauss 和留数，这给出阶为 q^{5g/6} 的显式项，其系数为实数且依赖 g 模 6，误差为 O_{q,ε}(q^{(23/28+ε)g})。该周期系数不恒为零。证明使用了函数方程提供的精确一阶差分、筛平均的 Euler 乘积，以及 Dirichlet 变量中的 Bochner 管定理。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $q$ be a fixed odd prime power with $q\equiv2\pmod3$. We study the first moment of primitive cubic Dirichlet $L$-functions over $\Fq(T)$, ordered by their genus. By averaging the rational sieve variable in the double Dirichlet series of Hong and Fang, we obtain meromorphic continuation beyond the secondary polar divisor. Combining this continuation with the cubic Gauss-sum residues computed by David, Florea and Lal\'in gives an explicit term of order $q^{5g/6}$, with a real coefficient depending on $g$ modulo $6$, and an error $O_{q,\eps}(q^{(23/28+\eps)g})$. The periodic coefficient is not identically zero. The proof uses an exact first difference supplied by the functional equation, an Euler product for the sieve average, and Bochner's tube theorem in the Dirichlet variables.

    📖 中文摘要

We also determine all solutions of $S_k(x)=y^n$ for $3\le x\le10^6$ with $x\equiv0,3\pmod4$, leaving $k$ and $n\ge2$ unrestricted and including squares. This extends Hajdu's fixed-$x$ classification in the same residue classes. A uniform restriction on the normalized odd part of $S_k(x)$ and finite certificates exclude every $k\ge6$; the remaining exponents are checked by exact integer arithmetic. The certificates admit independent verification. We further prove a limitation of congruence tests of bounded prime-power depth and solve the equation for every $x\equiv28\pmod{5\,110\,664}$.

    💡 证明数域与函数域上主导有理自映射的迭代高度上界，由第一动力次数与维数控制。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 K 为数域或曲线的函数域。设 f 为 K 上 d 维正规射影簇 X 的主导有理自映射，h_H 为大于等于 1 的丰沛 Weil 高度。设 λ_1(f) 表示 f 的第一动力次数。我们证明存在常数 C_{f,h_H} 大于 0，使得对任意 n 大于等于 1 及任意前向轨道有定义的 P 属于 X(K 的代数闭包)，若 λ_1(f) 大于 1，则 h_H(f^n(P)) 不超过 C_{f,h_H} h_H(P) n^{2(d-1)} λ_1(f)^n；若 λ_1(f) 等于 1，则 h_H(f^n(P)) 不超过 C_{f,h_H} h_H(P) n^{2d}。在数域上，证明结合了算术次数的新一致上界与点高度的一致比较；在函数域上，则依赖射影模型上几何次数相应的一致上界。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $K$ be a number field or a function field of a curve. Let $\lambda_1(f)$ denote the first dynamical degree of $f$. We show that there is a constant $C_{f,h_H}>0$ such that, for any $n\geq1$ and any point $P\in X(\overline{K})$ with well-defined forward orbit, $h_H(f^n(P))\leq C_{f,h_H}h_H(P)n^{2(d-1)}\lambda_1(f)^n$ if $\lambda_1(f)>1$, and $h_H(f^n(P))\leq C_{f,h_H}h_H(P)n^{2d}$ if $\lambda_1(f)=1$. Over number fields, the proof combines a new uniform upper bound for arithmetic degrees with a uniform comparison of point heights. Over function fields, it relies on the corresponding uniform upper bound for geometric degrees on a projective model.

  9. [2610.33631] 论 Lehmer 问题
    On Lehmer's Problem
    👥 Piyush Kumar Jha, R. Thangadurai
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究 Lehmer 问题中 Mahler 测度的下界。
    • 作者对内部系数落在固定剩余类或两类交替剩余类的互反不可约多项式给出显式下界。
    • 方法结合 Bilu 等分布定理的有效版本与 Borwein 等人的方法。
    • 结果解决了若干多项式族的 Lehmer 问题，并构造无穷多显式例子。
    💡 对内部系数落在固定剩余类中的首一互反不可约多项式，给出 Mahler 测度的显式下界，解决若干族的 Lehmer 问题。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 m 至少为 2 为整数，d 至少为 2 为偶数。我们研究首一、互反、非分圆多项式 P(x) 等于 x^d 加 a_{d-1}x^{d-1} 加至 a_1 x 加 1，系数为整数。对固定剩余类 r 属于 0 至 m-1，我们证明存在可有效计算的常数 c_{r,m} 大于 1，使得每个内部系数满足 a_j 模 m 余 r（1 小于等于 j 小于等于 d-1）的不可约多项式 Q 的 Mahler 测度 M(Q) 至少为 c_{r,m}。此外，我们用 r 与 m 显式算出 c_{r,m}。对剩余类 r 等于 2、r 等于 3 与 r 等于 m-1，我们在适当的模 m 范围内得到改进的下界。我们还考虑内部系数属于模 m 两个不同剩余类的首一、互反、不可约、非分圆多项式。更确切地说，若奇数下标内部系数模 m 余 a，偶数下标内部系数模 m 余 b，且 a 不模 m 同余于 b，则我们证明存在常数 c_{m,a,b} 大于 1 使 Mahler 测度 M(P) 至少为 c_{m,a,b}。这些结果解决了若干多项式族的 Lehmer 问题。我们还在每个偶次数 d 至少为 2 构造无穷多个 Q 上首一、互反、不可约、非分圆多项式，并给出其 Mahler 测度的显式信息。证明依赖 Bilu 的等分布定理及其由 D'Andrea 等人证明的有效版本，以及 Borwein 等人发展的方法。虽然将 Bilu 等分布结果与 Borwein 等人方法结合用于 Lehmer 问题很自然，但我们未见到文献中有先前工作采用这一特定途径。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $m\geq 2$ be an integer, and let $d\geq 2$ be an even integer. We study monic, reciprocal, non-cyclotomic polynomials $$ P(x)=x^d+a_{d-1}x^{d-1}+\cdots+a_1x+1\in\mathbb{Z}[x]. $$ For a fixed residue class $r\in\{0,1,\ldots,m-1\}$, we prove that there exists an effectively computable constant $c_{r,m}>1$ such that the Mahler measure $$ M(P)\geq c_{r,m} $$ for every irreducible polynomial $Q$ whose inner coefficients satisfy $$ a_j\equiv r\pmod m \qquad (1\leq j\leq d-1). $$ Moreover, we explicitly compute the constant $c_{r,m}$ in terms of $r$ and $m$. For the residue classes $r=2$, $r=3$, and $r=m-1$, we obtain improved lower bounds for suitable ranges of the modulus~$m$. We also consider monic, reciprocal, irreducible, non-cyclotomic polynomials whose inner coefficients belong to two distinct residue classes modulo $m$. More precisely, if the inner coefficients with odd indices are congruent to $a\pmod m$ and those with even indices are congruent to $b\pmod m$, where $a\not\equiv b\pmod m$, then we prove the existence of a constant $c_{m,a,b}>1$ such that the Mahler measure $$ M(P)\geq c_{m,a,b}. $$ These results solve Lehmer's problem for several families of polynomials. We also construct infinitely many examples in every even degree $d\geq 2$ of monic, reciprocal, irreducible, non-cyclotomic polynomials over $\mathbb{Q}$, together with explicit information about their Mahler measures. Our proofs rely on Y. Bilu's equidistribution theorem and its effective version proved by C. D'Andrea \emph{et al.}, as well as on a method developed by P. Borwein \emph{et al.} While it is natural to consider Bilu's equidistribution results in conjunction with the Borwein {\it et al}. method to Lehmer's problem, we are not aware of any previous work in the literature that employs this particular approach.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33631

    📖 中文摘要

The Ihara constant \begin{equation*} A(q)=\limsup_{g(X)\to\infty}\frac{N(X)}{g(X)} \end{equation*} measures the asymptotic abundance of rational points on curves over $\mathbb{F}_q$. In this paper, we study the Zassenhaus filtration of pro-$p$ groups arising from class field towers. By combining Golod--Shafarevich theorem, embedding problems and central class field theory, we establish a new criterion for the infinitude of class field towers. As an application, we obtain an improvement on the best known lower bounds of Ihara constant $A(2)$ and $A(3)$ which are explicitly given by \[ A(2)\geq 0.317779\cdots \text{ and } A(3)\geq 0.496074\cdots. \]

    💡 确定随机无幂次多项式定义的超椭圆曲线在多个扩域上仿射点计数的联合极限分布。
    📖 中文摘要

    固定有限域 Fq、与 q 互素的整数 m 至少为 2，以及 2 小于等于 κ 小于等于 m。对均匀选取的次数为 D 的 κ 次无幂次多项式 F 属于 Fq[x]，我们确定当 D 趋于无穷（q 与 n 固定）时，曲线 y^m 等于 F(x) 在 Fq 至 F_{q^n} 上仿射点计数的联合极限分布。其概率生成函数是带显式局部因子的有限 Euler 乘积。等价地，极限向量是独立闭点贡献之和，每个贡献可影响多个扩域次数。我们通过特化 Bertucci-Howe 的等分布与 motivic Euler 乘积形式得到该公式。随后，在取定次数极限后，我们计算当 q 在固定模 m 既约剩余类中变化时的均值、协方差与矩展开。单域特化可恢复 Cheong-Wood-Zaman 的无幂次仿射点计数分布。

    🌐 English Abstract

The probability generating function is a finite Euler product with explicit local factors. Equivalently, the limiting vector is a sum of independent closed-point contributions, each of which may affect several extension degrees. We obtain the formula by specializing the equidistribution and motivic Euler product formalism of Bertucci-Howe. We then compute means, covariances, and moment expansions as $q$ varies in a fixed reduced residue class modulo $m$, after the degree limit has been taken. The one-field specialization recovers the power-free affine point-count distribution of Cheong-Wood-Zaman.
