  12. [2610.33873] 素数因子与合数构成复杂网络的拓扑特征
    Exploring topological features of a complex network of prime factors and composite numbers
    👥 Carlos Daniel Tamayo-Castro, Aldo Ramirez-Arellano, Juan Bory-Reyes
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究素数因子与合数二部网络的拓扑性质。
    • 作者证明网络直径恒为 5，子网络最小直径为 4，故为小世界网络。
    • 并刻画边界节点、给出中心节点识别算法与熵维数计算。
    • 结果显示信息维数随规模下降，盒覆盖数趋于稳定。
    💡 证明素数因子-合数二部网络直径恒为 5，并刻画边界节点、中心节点算法与信息维数。
    📖 中文摘要

    素数因其在整数中的相对稀少、出现与密度的相对不可预测而成为特别受关注的数学概念。它们构成所有其他合数的基石，引出素数分解这一由算术基本定理保证的重要概念。在已报道的关于素数及其与合数相互关系的若干工作中，一些考虑了导出图或网络。本文推导素数因子与合数构成的二部复杂网络的若干拓扑特征。更具体地说，我们数学地证明该网络直径恰为 5，与节点数无关。特别地，我们证明某些非平凡子网络的最小直径等于 4，只要网络足够大，这也与节点数无关。后者表明该网络是小世界型的。我们还精确刻画网络中边界节点的数目，并给出若干算法以识别网络中最重要、最相关、最有影响或最中心的节点。最后，我们基于 Shannon 熵与 Tsallis 熵计算所研究复杂网络的信息维数。结果表明两者均随节点数增加而减小。此外，两种熵都表明在直径 ε 等于 3、4 的盒覆盖下，节点被分成大致相同数目的盒子，与网络节点数无关。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Prime numbers are mathematical concepts of particular interest because of their relative rarity among integers, as well as the relative unpredictability of their occurrence and density along the integer sequence. They constitute the building blocks of all other composite numbers, giving rise to the interesting and important concept of prime factorization, a process guaranteed by the fundamental theorem of arithmetic. Among the several works that have been reported about prime numbers and their interrelationship with composite numbers, some have considered derived graphs and/or networks. In this paper, we derive some topological features of a bipartite complex network of prime factors and composite numbers. More specifically, we prove mathematically that the network's diameter is exactly five regardless of the number of nodes. In particular, we show that certain nontrivial sub-networks have minimal diameter equal to four, which is, again, independent of the number of nodes as long as the network is large enough. The last shows that this network is of small-world type. We also characterize exactly the number of boundary nodes in the network and present a couple of algorithms to identify the most important, relevant, influential, or central nodes of the network. Finally, we focus on the computation of the information dimensions on the basis of Shannon and Tsallis entropies of the studied complex network. The results show both decreasing as the number of nodes increases.   14. [2610.34160] Catalan 数立方和的有限和恒等式与超同余
    Finite-sum Identities and Supercongruences for Cubes of Catalan Numbers
    👥 Yuqi Liu
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究 Catalan 数立方和的超同余。
    • 作者建立精确有限和恒等式，结合二项式超同余与 Sun 的约化。
    • 得到两个模 p^3 的显式超同余及加权版本。
    • 方法依赖相邻项之比、双和对称性与裂项，端点项处理是关键。
    💡 建立 Catalan 数立方有限和恒等式，导出两个模 p^3 的加权超同余。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 C_k 等于 binom(2k,k)/(k+1) 为第 k 个 Catalan 数，p 为奇素数。我们建立精确的有限和恒等式，结合已知二项式超同余与 Sun 的约化，得到当 p 等于 x^2 加 3y^2 模 3 余 1 时，从 k 等于 0 到 (p-1)/2 求和 C_k^3/16^k 模 p^3 同余于 2 减 24y^2 减 5p^2/(2y^2)；当 p 等于 x^2 加 4y^2 模 4 余 1 时，从 k 等于 0 到 (p-1)/2 求和 C_k^3/(-8)^k 模 p^3 同余于 -1 减 48y^2 减 33p^2/(16y^2)。我们还计算分母为 16^k(k+1)^2 的相应和，得到两个加权 Catalan 超同余。有限恒等式来自相邻项之比、双和的对称性与裂项。所有端点项在模约化前保留在有理数上。在每个双线性恒等式中，一个端点分母等于 p，其消去产生所需的 p^2 阶项。精确的算术输入另行陈述。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $C_k=\binom{2k}{k}/(k+1)$ be the $k$-th Catalan number, and let $p$ be an odd prime. We establish exact finite-sum identities that, together with known binomial supercongruences and reductions of Sun, yield $\sum_{k=0}^{(p-1)/2}\frac{C_k^3}{16^k}\equiv2-24y^2-\frac{5p^2}{2y^2}\pmod{p^3}$ when $p=x^2+3y^2\equiv1\pmod3$, and $\sum_{k=0}^{(p-1)/2}\frac{C_k^3}{(-8)^k}\equiv-1-48y^2-\frac{33p^2}{16y^2}\pmod{p^3}$ when $p=x^2+4y^2\equiv1\pmod4$. We also evaluate the corresponding sum with denominator $16^k(k+1)^2$, and obtain two weighted Catalan supercongruences. The finite identities follow from the ratios of consecutive summands, symmetry of double sums, and telescoping. All endpoint terms are retained over the rational numbers before any modular reduction. In each bilinear identity, one endpoint denominator equals $p$; its cancellation produces the necessary term of order $p^2$. The precise arithmetic inputs are stated separately.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34160
  15. [2610.34288] 交叉数与用长度集刻画有限阿贝尔群
    Cross numbers and the characterization of finite abelian groups by length sets
    👥 Qinghai Zhong
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究有限阿贝尔群能否由长度集刻画的反问题。
    • 作者对所有同循环群 C_n^r 给出肯定回答。
    • 并证明最小零和序列最大交叉数的精确公式。
    • 结果将零和理论与半阶乘性联系，推进了非唯一分解理论的核心问题。
    💡 解决同循环群 C_n^r 的刻画问题，并给出最小零和序列最大交叉数的精确公式。
    📖 中文摘要

    刻画问题问：Davenport 常数至少为 4 的有限阿贝尔群是否在同构意义下由其长度集系统决定。这是非唯一分解理论中的核心反问题：它问可能的不可约因子个数是否保留足够信息以恢复底层类群。对代数数域的整数环，肯定回答意味着理想类群可仅由这些分解长度恢复。我们对所有同循环群 C_n^r 解决该问题：若 G 为有限阿贝尔群且 D(G) 至少为 4、L(G) 等于 L(C_n^r)，则 G 同构于 C_n^r。序列的交叉数是其各项阶的倒数之和。这一序列长度的加权度量联系零和理论与半阶乘性，确定其在最小零和序列上的最大值是一个长期极值问题。我们还得到在最小距离被环境群秩减一整除的子集上，最小零和序列最大交叉数的精确公式。对 G 同构于 C_{n_1} 直和至 C_{n_r}，其中 1 小于 n_1 整除至 n_r 且 r 至少为 max{2, n_r-2}，我们证明 K(G,r-1) 等于 1 加 (r-1) 乘以对 n_1 的素因子 p 求和 (1 减 p 的负 v_p(n_1) 次方)，这是解决同循环群刻画问题的关键要素。

    🌐 English Abstract

    The Characterization Problem asks whether a finite abelian group with Davenport constant at least four is determined, up to isomorphism, by its system of length sets. This is a central inverse problem in non-unique factorization theory: it asks whether the possible numbers of irreducible factors retain enough information to recover the underlying class group. For rings of integers of algebraic number fields, an affirmative answer means that the ideal class group can be recovered from these factorization lengths alone. We resolve this problem for all homocyclic groups $C_n^r$: if $G$ is a finite abelian group with $\mathsf D(G)\ge4$ and $\mathcal L(G)=\mathcal L(C_n^r)$, then $G\cong C_n^r$. The cross number of a sequence is the sum of the reciprocals of the orders of its terms. This weighted measure of sequence length links zero-sum theory with half-factoriality, and determining its maximal value for minimal zero-sum sequences is a longstanding extremal problem. We also obtain an exact formula for the maximal cross number of minimal zero-sum sequences over subsets whose minimum distance is divisible by the rank of the ambient group minus one. For $G\cong C_{n_1}\oplus\cdots\oplus C_{n_r}$ with $1<n_1\mid\cdots\mid n_r$ and $r\ge\max\{2,n_r-2\}$, we prove \[ \mathsf K(G,r-1)=1+(r-1)\sum_{p\mid n_1} \left(1-p^{-\mathsf v_p(n_1)}\right), \] which is a key ingredient in resolving the Characterization Problem for homocyclic groups.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34288

    Frangible prime gaps

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 论文研究易碎素数间隙的计数常数 c。

    • 并完成 10^17 以内易碎间隙的完整普查，经验值与估计吻合。
    • 结果证实了 Gafni-Tao 的猜测。
    💡 改进易碎素数间隙常数 c 的估计至区间 [2.7416, 2.7984]，并给出 10^17 以内的完整普查。
    📖 中文摘要

    若每个满足 p_n 小于 m 小于 p_{n+1} 的整数 m 的最小素因子都严格小于间隙大小 p_{n+1} 减 p_n，则称素数间隙 (p_n, p_{n+1}) 为易碎的。Gafni 与 Tao 最近证明直到 X 的易碎间隙数目远小于 X/log^2 X，回答了 Erdős 的一个问题。他们还在 Dickson-Hardy-Littlewood 素数元组猜想下证明该数目渐近于 c X/log^2 X，其中 c 为常数，并给出粗略估计。我们在同一假设下描述改进的估计 c 的方法，并确立其值位于 [2.7416, 2.7984]，证实了 Gafni 与 Tao 的猜测。此外，我们给出直到 10^17 的易碎间隙完整普查结果。随后的 c 的经验测量与我们的估计非常吻合。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A prime gap $(p_n, p_{n+1})$ is \textit{frangible} if the smallest prime divisor of every integer $m$ with $p_n<m<p_{n+1}$ is strictly less than the size of the gap, $p_{n+1}-p_n$. Gafni and Tao recently proved that the number of frangible gaps up to $X$ is $\ll X/\log^2 X$, answering a question of Erd\H{o}s. They also showed that under the Dickson--Hardy--Littlewood prime tuples conjecture this number is $\sim c X/\log^2 X$ for a constant $c$, for which they obtained a rough estimate. We describe improved methods for estimating this constant under the same hypothesis, and establish that its value lies in $[2.7416, 2.7984]$, confirming a speculation of Gafni and Tao. In addition, we present the results of a complete survey of frangible gaps up to $10^{17}$. An ensuing empirical measurement of $c$ matches our estimate very well.

    Admissible sets of integers in imaginary quadratic fields of class number 1
    👥 Daniel Shiu
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究类数 1 的虚二次域整数环中相对直径较大的容许集。

    • 得到所论域中素数间隙的新记录。

    💡 计算研究类数 1 虚二次域整数环中相对直径较大的容许集，给出素数间隙新记录及第二 Hardy-Littlewood 猜想反例。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 O_K 为数域 K 的整数环。有限子集 H={η_1,…,η_k}⊂O_K 称为容许的，若对 O_K 中每个素理想 p，η_i 模 p 的剩余类不覆盖所有剩余类。有限子集 S 的直径定义为 diam(S):=max_{η_i≠η_j}|N(η_i−η_j)|，其中 N 为到有理域的范数。本文计算研究类数为 1 的虚二次域（如高斯整数环或艾森斯坦整数环）整数环中相对直径较大的容许子集。动机有二：一是改进类似于大素数间最小间隙上界问题的结果，这是 21 世纪数论的重大成就之一；二是借此有机会否证第二 Hardy-Littlewood 猜想的一个变体。计算给出了所论域中素数间最小距离的新记录，并在除一个域外的所有域中给出第二 Hardy-Littlewood 猜想推广的显式反例。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $\mathcal O_K$ be the ring of integers of a number field $K$. A finite subset $\mathcal H=\{\eta_1,\ldots,\eta_k\}\subset\mathcal O_K$ is said to be \emph{admissible} if for every prime ideal $\mathfrak p$ in $\mathcal O_K$, the reductions of the $\eta_i$ modulo $\mathfrak p$ do not represent every residue class. The diameter of a finite subset $S$ of $\mathcal O_K$ is defined to be \[\mathrm{diam}(S):= \max_{\eta_i,\eta_j\in S:\eta_i\neq\eta_j}|\mathcal N(\eta_i-\eta_j)|\] where $\mathcal N$ denotes the norm down to the rational field. This paper computationally investigates admissible subsets of rings of integers in imaginary quadratic fields of class number 1 (such as the Gaussian integers or the Eisenstein integers) that are large relative to their diameter. The motivation is twofold. Firstly, there is the improvement of results analogous to the question of an upper bound for the least gap between large rational primes, which has been one of the great number theoretic successes of the twenty-first century. A second motivation is that there is the opportunity to disprove a variation of the second Hardy-Littlewood conjecture. The calculations in the paper provide new records on the smallest distance between primes in the fields in question, and explicit counter-examples to a generalisation of the second Hardy-Littlewood conjecture in all bar one of the fields.

    🤖 AI 总结
    • 在全实数域上引入迹欧几里得与 house 欧几里得二次格概念。
    • 借助 p-范数欧几里得性质建立这些概念之间的联系。
    • 证明任意次数全实数域上正定迹欧几里得格在同构意义下仅有有限多个。
    • 分类实二次域上秩一迹欧几里得格，恰有六个同构类且均为自由格。
    • 由此确定迹欧几里得实二次域恰为 Q(√2)、Q(√5)、Q(√13)。
    💡 引入迹欧几里得与 house 欧几里得二次格，证明任意次数全实数域上正定迹欧几里得格仅有有限多个同构类，并分类实二次域秩一格。
    📖 中文摘要

    受 Clark 关于欧几里得二次型工作的启发，我们在全实数域上引入迹欧几里得与 house 欧几里得二次格，并通过 p-范数欧几里得性质联系这些概念。我们证明：当 F 取遍任意次数的全实数域时，任意秩 n ≥ 1 的正定迹欧几里得 O_F-格在同构意义下只有有限多个。对每个 p ∈ [1,∞]，正定 p-范数欧几里得格也满足同样的有限性。我们还分类了实二次域上秩一的正定迹欧几里得格：恰有六个同构类，且每个这样的格都是自由的。作为推论，迹欧几里得实二次域恰为 Q(√2)、Q(√5) 和 Q(√13)。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Motivated by Clark's work on Euclidean quadratic forms, we introduce trace Euclidean and house Euclidean quadratic lattices over totally real number fields, and relate these concepts through the $p$-norm Euclidean property. We prove that, as $F$ varies over all totally real number fields of arbitrary degree, there are only finitely many positive definite trace Euclidean $\mathcal{O}_{F}$-lattices of arbitrary rank $n\ge1$, up to isometry. For every $p\in[1,\infty]$, the same finiteness holds for positive definite $p$-norm Euclidean lattices. We also classify all positive definite trace Euclidean lattices of rank one over real quadratic fields: there are exactly six isometry classes, and every such lattice is free. As a corollary, the trace Euclidean real quadratic fields are precisely $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{2})$, $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{5})$, and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt{13})$.

    👥 Thang Pham, Dung The Tran, Boqing Xue
    🤖 AI 总结

    • 定义最大无等比距离集大小 M_{GP,d}(p) 并建立奇维与一维情形之间的定量关系。
    • 证明对 p ≥ 7 有 p^m M_{GP,1}(p) ≤ M_{GP,2m+1}(p) ≤ 4p^m M_{GP,1}(p)。
    • 对大素数进一步得到指数介于 7/26 与 16/25 之间的精细上下界。
    • 这些结果刻画了距离集中乘法结构的普遍性。
    💡 研究奇维素域距离集中非退化三项等比数列的存在性，给出最大无等比距离集大小的上下界。
    📖 中文摘要

    对 E⊂F_p^d，令 Δ(E)={Q_d(x−y):x,y∈E}，其中 Q_d(z)=z_1^2+…+z_d^2。我们引入并研究当 d 为奇数时距离集 Δ(E) 中非退化三项等比数列的存在性问题。若 M_{GP,d}(p) 表示距离集不含此类等比数列的集合的最大大小，则对每个素数 p ≥ 7 和整数 m ≥ 0，有 p^m M_{GP,1}(p) ≤ M_{GP,2m+1}(p) ≤ 4p^m M_{GP,1}(p)。对所有充分大的素数 p，我们还证明 p^{m+7/26} ≤ M_{GP,2m+1}(p) ≤ C p^{m+16/25}，其中 C 为绝对正常数。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For $E\subset\mathbb{F}_p^d$, let $\Delta(E)=\{Q_d(x-y):x,y\in E\}$, where $Q_d(z)=z_1^2+\cdots+z_d^2$. We introduce and study the problem of the existence of nondegenerate three-term geometric progressions in the distance set $\Delta(E)$ when $d$ is odd. If $M_{GP,d}(p)$ denotes the largest size of a set whose distance set contains no such progression, then, for every prime $p\geq7$ and every integer $m\geq0$, \[ p^mM_{GP,1}(p)\leq M_{GP,2m+1}(p) \leq4p^mM_{GP,1}(p). \] For all sufficiently large primes $p$, we also prove \[ p^{m+\frac{7}{26}}\leq M_{GP,2m+1}(p) \leq Cp^{m+\frac{16}{25}}, \] where $C$ is an absolute positive constant.

  24. [2610.34808] 不同素数 q,p 对应的族 X_{qp^s} 与 Carmichael 欧拉函数猜想
    The Family $X_{qp^s}$ for Distinct Primes $q,p$ in Connection with Carmichael's Totient Conjecture
    👥 Tian-Xiao He
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究欧拉函数原像集 X_k 与 Carmichael 猜想的联系。
    • 完全解决关于 X_{2p^s} 的猜想，给出所有 s 的刻画并发现奇偶二分现象。
    • 补上 p=3 的缺口，单独刻画 X_{2·3^s}。
    • 将问题推广到 X_{qp^s}，按 q、p 的奇偶性分为三种情形。
    • 完整解决 q=2 与 p=2、s=2 的情形，但镜像族 s ≥ 3 仍开放。
    💡 完全解决 X_{2p^s} 的刻画问题并给出奇偶二分，进而分析一般族 X_{qp^s} 的三种情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    Shannon、Shiue、Saito 与作者研究了集合 X_k={n∈N:φ(n)=k} 与 Carmichael 欧拉函数猜想的关系，证明了对于奇素数 p≠3 和 s∈{1,2,3,4}，当 2p^s+1 不是素数时 X_{2p^s} 为空（s=1 情形已双向完全刻画），并猜想这对所有 s ≥ 1 成立。我们完整解决该猜想，对每个 s ≥ 1 和每个奇素数 p≠3 给出 X_{2p^s} 的完全刻画，并得到奇偶二分：s 为偶数时无条件有 X_{2p^s}=∅；s 为奇数时两种结果都确实出现，且恰由 2p^s+1 的素性决定。随后我们补上 p=3 时证明技巧退化的唯一缺口，单独给出 X_{2·3^s} 的无条件刻画。在此基础上考虑自然推广 X_{qp^s}，其中 q≠p 为不同素数、s ≥ 1。我们证明该推广分为三种性质迥异的情形：若 q 与 p 均为奇数，问题平凡，qp^s 为奇数故由初等奇偶性得 X_{qp^s}=∅；若 q=2，问题正是上述已解决的族 X_{2p^s}；若 p=2，得到「镜像」族 X_{2^s q}，问题本质不同。除 s=1 外，我们完整解决 s=2 情形，以显式证明和解集恢复文献中的定性二分，但 s ≥ 3 仍开放。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Shannon, Shiue, Saito and the author \cite{SSHS2025} studied the sets $X_k=\{n\in\mathbb{N}:\varphi(n)=k\}$ in connection with Carmichael's totient conjecture, and proved that for an odd prime $p\neq3$ and $s\in\{1,2,3,4\}$, $X_{2p^s}$ is empty whenever $2p^s+1$ is not prime (with $s=1$ fully characterized in both directions), conjecturing that this persists for every $s\ge1$. We resolve that conjecture in full, obtaining a complete characterization of $X_{2p^s}$ for every $s\ge1$ and every odd prime $p\neq3$, together with a parity dichotomy: $X_{2p^s}=\varnothing$ unconditionally whenever $s$ is even, while for odd $s$ both outcomes genuinely occur, determined exactly by the primality of $2p^s+1$. We then close the one remaining gap at $p=3$, where the proof technique degenerates, with a separate, unconditional characterization of $X_{2\cdot3^s}$. Building on these results, we consider the natural generalization $X_{qp^s}$, where $q\neq p$ are distinct primes and $s\ge1$. We show that this generalization splits into three cases of very different character. If $q$ and $p$ are both odd, the problem is trivial: $qp^s$ is odd, so $X_{qp^s}=\varnothing$ by elementary parity, with no case-analysis needed. If $q=2$, the problem is exactly the family $X_{2p^s}$ resolved above. If $p=2$, giving the ``mirror'' family $X_{2^sq}$ for an odd prime $q$, the problem is genuinely different. Beyond $s=1$, we give a complete resolution for $s=2$, recovering, with an explicit proof and solution sets, the qualitative dichotomy of \cite[Thm.~2.3.1]{SSHS2025}, but the problem remains open for $s\ge3$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34808

    🌐 English Abstract

Using this update, we establish a transcendence characterization of equicharacteristic local shtukas with abelian Tannakian structure group, matching a result of Howe--Klevdal in the $p$-adic setting. A special case of this result says that a formal Drinfeld module over an equicharacteristic local field has complex multiplication if and only if the Hodge--Tate--Taguchi filtration on its $z$-adic Tate module is algebraic.

    👥 Wouter van Doorn, Boning Liu, Quanyu Tang
    🤖 AI 总结

    💡 证明几乎完美加法补集的 limsup A(x)B(x)/x 落在 [3/2,2]，下界尖锐并证实 Chen 的猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $A, B$ be infinite sets of non-negative integers with $n\in A+B$ for all sufficiently large integers $n$. We prove that if $A(x)B(x)-x$ does not tend to infinity, then $$\limsup_{x\to\infty}\frac{A(x)B(x)}{x}\in\Big[\frac{3}{2}, 2\Big].$$ The lower bound is sharp, and confirms a conjecture of Chen. Our proof is based on the weak limits and the convolution factorization theorem of Gabardo and Lai. A discrete proof of the same bound was also given by Zhang. Motivated by the known spectrum of perfect additive complements, we conjecture that the interval $(3/2, 8/5)$ is also excluded for almost perfect additive complements.

  30. [2610.35030] 固定数域上多项式的 Galois 群：一般盒中的行列式方法、预解式与定量计数
    Galois groups of polynomials over a fixed number field: determinant methods in general boxes, resolvents, and quantitative counting
    👥 Ilaria Viglino
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 在固定数域上研究不等坐标盒中整点的行列式方法估计。
    • 证明仿射平面曲线的 Bombieri-Pila 型估计与仿射曲面的 Salberger-Browning 覆盖定理的数域类比。
    • 将这些估计应用于首一多项式 Galois 预解式的定量计数。
    • 结合大筛与有效 Hilbert 不可约性得到例外多项式的上界及子群敏感界。
    • 所有行列式估计均在文中证明，不依赖猜想性命题。
    💡 在不等坐标盒中建立数域版行列式方法估计，并应用于多项式 Galois 预解式的定量计数。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 K 为次数 d 的固定数域。我们证明两个关于 O_K 上整点、坐标可有不同大小界的一般盒中的行列式方法结果：一个保留两个独立坐标界的仿射平面曲线 Bombieri-Pila 型估计，以及一个仿射曲面的 Salberger-Browning 覆盖定理。若 F∈O_K[X_1,X_2] 不可约，且 T=max B_1^{e_1}B_2^{e_2} 取遍 F 中出现的单项式，则 N_K(F;B_1,B_2)≪T^ε exp(d log B_1 log B_2 / log T)。对三变量绝对不可约曲面，我们得到由 O(T^ε V_{3,K}) 个有界次数辅助超曲面构成的覆盖，例外整点数为 O(T^ε V_{3,K}^2)，其中 V_{3,K}=exp(d (log B_1 log B_2 log B_3 / log T)^{1/2})。这些是有理情形下 Browning 与 Heath-Brown、Salberger、Browning、Chow-Dietmann 发展的不等坐标范围行列式方法估计的数域类比。随后我们将它们应用于 K 上首一 n 次多项式的 Galois 预解式。独立地，遵循 Gallagher 概率 Galois 理论传统的大筛论证给出 O_{K,n}(N^{dn−d/2} log N) 个例外可分多项式，而整体域上的有效 Hilbert 不可约性给出以指数 d(n−1+1/[S_n:G]) 为特征的子群敏感界（至多对数因子）。我们记录了与偶群判别式平方方程、同余限制及矩形系数盒的相互作用。应用中使用的所有行列式估计均在文中证明，不假设任何猜想性的整体域行列式命题。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $K$ be a fixed number field of degree $d$. We prove two determinant-method results for integral points over $\mathcal O_K$ in boxes whose coordinates may have different size bounds: a Bombieri-Pila type estimate for affine plane curves that retains the two separate coordinate bounds, and a Salberger-Browning covering theorem for affine surfaces. If $F\in\mathcal O_K[X_1,X_2]$ is irreducible and $T=\max B_1^{e_1}B_2^{e_2}$ over the monomials occurring in $F$, then \[ N_K(F;B_1,B_2)\ll T^\varepsilon \exp\!\left(d\frac{\log B_1\log B_2}{\log T}\right). \] For an absolutely irreducible surface in three variables we obtain a covering by $O(T^\varepsilon V_{3,K})$ bounded-degree auxiliary hypersurfaces, outside $O(T^\varepsilon V_{3,K}^2)$ exceptional integral points, where \[ V_{3,K}=\exp\!\left(d \left(\frac{\log B_1\log B_2\log B_3}{\log T}\right)^{1/2}\right). \] These are the number-field analogues of determinant-method estimates for unequal coordinate ranges developed in the rational setting by Browning and Heath-Brown, Salberger, Browning, and Chow-Dietmann. We then apply them to Galois resolvents of monic degree-$n$ polynomials over $K$. Independently, a large-sieve argument, in the tradition of Gallagher's probabilistic Galois theory, gives $O_{K,n}(N^{dn-d/2}\log N)$ exceptional separable polynomials, while effective Hilbert irreducibility over global fields gives subgroup-sensitive bounds with exponent $d(n-1+1/[S_n:G])$, up to logarithms. We record the interaction with the discriminant-square equation for even groups, congruence restrictions, and rectangular coefficient boxes. All determinant estimates used in the applications are proved in the paper; no conjectural global-field determinant statement is assumed.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35030

    • 基于扭曲方法建立素理想权重可变的加权密度判据。
    • 在临界 Mertens 情形得到比先前更小的容许权重 1+(log Np)^3 (log log Np)^{1+ε}/Np。
    • 将解析假设纳入 Knopfmacher 的 Axiom A 框架，并在次临界范围证明大模覆盖比例任意小。
    • 指出扭曲构造可推广到更一般的剩余空间系统，且矩层级不能降低对数指数 3。
    💡 在 Dedekind 域上建立加权密度判据，改进临界 Mertens 情形下的素理想权重并纳入 Axiom A 框架。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究具有有限剩余范数的 Dedekind 域上有限族剩余类所产生的未覆盖集的密度。基于 Balister、Bollobás、Morris、Sahasrabudhe 与 Tiba 的扭曲方法，我们证明了一个一般加权密度判据，其中赋予素理想的权重可以变化。在临界 Mertens 情形下，该判据对每个 ε>0 给出容许的素理想权重 1+(log Np)^3 (log log Np)^{1+ε}/Np，至多在有限多个小素理想处作无害修改。这渐近地小于先前密度定理中出现的固定权重 1+(log Np)^{3+ε}/Np。结果对任意固定重数的系统成立；对重数一，要求理想模本身互异但不要求其范数互异。随后我们将解析假设置于 Knopfmacher 的 Axiom A 框架中。在指数为 1 的 Axiom A 临界情形下，加权定理所需的素理想估计自动成立。更一般地，在指数 ρ>0 的 Axiom A 下，我们证明理想上的加权均值定理；在次临界范围 0<ρ<1 中，一阶矩论证表明充分大的模覆盖任意小比例的环境空间。我们还说明扭曲构造本身只使用具有一致纤维和类中国剩余乘积结构的相容有限剩余空间系统，提示可能推广到 Dedekind 域之外。最后，我们证明在此矩层级内从二阶矩直接过渡到更高整数阶矩不会降低对数指数 3。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the density of uncovered sets arising from finite families of residue classes over Dedekind domains with finite residue norms. Building on the distortion method of Balister, Bollob\'as, Morris, Sahasrabudhe and Tiba, we prove a general weighted density criterion in which the weight attached to a prime ideal is allowed to vary. In the critical Mertens regime this yields, for every \(\varepsilon>0\), the admissible prime-ideal weight \[ 1+\frac{(\log N\mathfrak p)^3(\log\log N\mathfrak p)^{1+\varepsilon}}{N\mathfrak p}, \] up to a harmless modification at finitely many small prime ideals. This is asymptotically smaller than the fixed weight \(1+(\log N\mathfrak p)^{3+\varepsilon}/N\mathfrak p\) appearing in the earlier density theorem. The result is valid for systems of any fixed multiplicity and, for multiplicity one, requires the ideal moduli themselves to be distinct but not their norms. We then place the analytic hypotheses in Knopfmacher's Axiom~A framework. In the critical case of Axiom~A with exponent \(1\), the prime-ideal estimates needed by the weighted theorem are automatic. More generally, under Axiom~A with exponent \(\rho>0\), we prove a weighted mean-value theorem on ideals; in the subcritical range \(0<\rho<1\), a first-moment argument shows that sufficiently large moduli cover an arbitrarily small proportion of the ambient space. We also explain that the distortion construction itself only uses a compatible system of finite residue spaces with uniform fibres and a Chinese-remainder-type product structure, suggesting a possible extension beyond Dedekind domains. Finally, we show that a direct passage from the second moment to higher integer moments does not lower the logarithmic exponent \(3\) within this moment hierarchy.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35139

From this result, we also show that there are at most $7$ distinct squares when $y_{0}=1,2^{2},\ldots,7^{2}$, or once $d$ exceeds an explicit lower bound, without any conditions on the size of such squares.

    📖 中文摘要

We prove infinite families of congruences modulo powers of $7$ for $a_k(n)$ when $k \in \{4,5,7\}$. For each positive integer $\alpha$, let $\lambda_{k,\alpha}$ be the least nonnegative solution of $24\lambda_{k,\alpha}\equiv-(k-2)\pmod{7^\alpha}$. Then, we show that \[ a_k(7^\alpha n+\lambda_{k,\alpha}) \equiv 0\pmod{7^{\lfloor(\alpha+1)/2\rfloor}} \] for every nonnegative integer $n$. These results extend congruences modulo $7$ of Hirschhorn and Sellers (2026) and complement Chen's (2025) congruences for the reciprocal crank parity function $a_3(n)$.

  34. [2610.35306] 正特征中的谱 zeta 函数
    Spectral zeta functions in positive characteristic
    👥 Su Hu, Min-Soo Kim
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 在正特征局部域上研究谱 zeta 函数与正则化行列式。
    • 指出复幂指数仅在 Z_p 中连续变化、标量迹仅模 p 记录重数两大障碍。
    • 用逆 Stieltjes 对偶将预解式转化为局部解析谱分布，并以 Carlitz 矩增长条件刻画可实现幂级数。
    • 用 Frobenius 缺陷替代迹，刻画对角谱并给出移位 Carlitz 算子的 Fredholm 行列式。
    • 精确确定该行列式的牛顿多边形，建立正特征谱理论框架。
    💡 在正特征局部域上发展基于预解式的谱 zeta 理论，分别处理复幂指数与标量迹的两类障碍。
    📖 中文摘要

    谱 zeta 函数与 zeta 正则化行列式用解析函数编码算子的谱。在 F_q((π)) 的代数闭包的完备化 C_∞ 上，特征 p 的两个特性打破了这一经典图景：复幂的指数只在 Z_p 中连续变化而非解析变化；标量迹只模 p 记录特征值重数。我们发展了一套基于预解式的理论，分别处理这两个障碍。对第一个障碍，逆 Stieltjes 对偶将合适的标量预解式转化为 F_q[[π]] 上的局部解析谱分布。Mellin 变换的单个指数就已决定该分布及整个 Mellin 族。我们通过 Carlitz 矩的增长条件精确刻画了哪些幂级数以此方式出现，该判据依赖于支撑集：有界 Taylor 系数并不充分。对平稳数字测度，变换是显式压缩映射的唯一不动点，前 q−1 个非常数矩可恢复该测度。对第二个障碍，我们用 Frobenius 缺陷 F/σ(F)（σ 将系数提升到 q 次幂）替代迹。在系数较小的幂级数上，该映射是保持 Gauss 距离的群自同构，且收敛无穷乘积可将其求逆。多项式缺陷产生整函数，它们精确刻画了有限个前向 Frobenius 轨道并集（计重数）的对角谱。对移位 Carlitz 算子，所得 Fredholm 行列式是 Anderson-Thakur 函数的规范化倒数，其牛顿多边形被精确确定。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Spectral zeta functions and zeta-regularized determinants encode the spectrum of an operator in analytic functions. Over the completion $\mathbb C_\infty$ of an algebraic closure of $\mathbb F_q((\pi))$, two features of characteristic $p$ break this classical picture. Complex powers have exponents that vary only continuously in $\mathbb Z_p$, not analytically. Scalar traces record eigenvalue multiplicities only modulo $p$. We develop a resolvent-based theory that treats these two obstructions separately. For the first, inverse Stieltjes duality turns suitable scalar resolvents into locally analytic spectral distributions on $\mathbb F_q[[\pi]]$. A single exponent of the Mellin transform already determines the distribution and the entire Mellin family. We characterize exactly which power series arise in this way, by a growth condition on Carlitz moments. The criterion depends on the support: bounded Taylor coefficients are not sufficient. For stationary digit measures, the transform is the unique fixed point of an explicit contraction. The first $q-1$ nonconstant moments recover the measure. For the second, we replace traces by the Frobenius defect $F/\sigma(F)$, where $\sigma$ raises coefficients to their $q$th powers. On power series with small coefficients, this map is a group automorphism that preserves Gauss distances, and a convergent infinite product inverts it. Polynomial defects produce entire functions. They characterize exactly the diagonal spectra that are finite unions of forward Frobenius orbits, counted with multiplicity. For the shifted Carlitz operator, the resulting Fredholm determinant is a normalized reciprocal of the Anderson--Thakur function, and its Newton polygon is determined exactly.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35306

    💡 证明数域上拟射影簇与其 Weil 限制的 Brauer-Manin 集一致，包括 Br1 情形。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 X 是数域 k 上的拟射影簇，X' 是它关于有限域扩张 k/k' 的 Weil 限制。我们证明 X 的 Brauer-Manin 集与 X' 的 Brauer-Manin 集一致。我们也证明由 Br1 给出的 Brauer-Manin 集的一致性。

    🌐 English Abstract

    For a quasi-projective variety $X$ defined over a number field $k$, let $X'$ be its Weil restriction with respect to a finite field extension $k/k'$. We prove that the Brauer--Manin set of $X$ agrees with the Brauer--Manin set of $X'$. We also prove the agreement for Brauer--Manin set of them given by $\mathrm{Br}_1$.

  42. [2610.33791] MacMahon 与 Andrews 两个分拆函数中钩数的 inequalities 与渐近
    Inequalities and asymptotics for hook numbers in two partition functions of MacMahon and Andrews
    👥 Rupam Barman, Archit Deb, Sayantan Ghorai
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究 MacMahon 与 Andrews 两类分拆中固定长度钩的总数。
    • 用 Wright 圆法导出长度 1、2、3 钩的生成函数与渐近公式。
    • 由此得到两类分拆间及类内不同钩长的偏差规律。
    • 并证明随 r 增大 B 型分拆的小钩数单调占优。
    💡 给出两类分拆中长度 1、2、3 钩总数的生成函数与渐近公式，导出钩长偏差结果。
    📖 中文摘要

    受限制分拆中钩长偏差近期研究的启发，我们研究 MacMahon 与 Andrews 引入的两个分拆函数中固定长度钩的总数。1916 年 MacMahon 证明：n 的无重数一部分拆数等于 n 的偶部或模 6 余 3 部分的分拆数。对正整数 r，令 A_r(n) 计数 n 的每个出现奇数次的部至少出现 2r+1 次的分拆，B_r(n) 计数部为偶数或模 4r+2 余 2r+1 的分拆。Andrews 证明对所有 r 与 n 有 A_r(n)=B_r(n)，r=1 即 MacMahon 恒等式。我们用 Wright 圆法导出两类分拆中长度 1、2、3 钩总数的生成函数与渐近公式。这些公式给出若干钩长偏差结果：充分大 n 时 B_r 型分拆的 1-钩多于 A_r 型；钩长 2、3 时 r=1 偏差反向，r≥2 时偏向 B_r 型。我们还建立类内不同长度钩数的渐近不等式，并证明固定钩长 ℓ∈{1,2,3} 时 B_r 型分拆的 ℓ-钩多于 B_{r+1} 型。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Motivated by recent studies of hook length biases in restricted partitions, we investigate the total number of hooks of fixed length in two partition functions introduced by MacMahon and Andrews. In $1916$, MacMahon proved that the number of partitions of $n$ in which no part appears with multiplicity one equals the number of partitions of $n$ into parts that are even or congruent to $3 \pmod{6}$. For a positive integer $r$, let $A_r(n)$ count the partitions of $n$ in which every part occurring an odd number of times occurs at least $2r+1$ times, and let $B_r(n)$ count the partitions into parts that are even or congruent to $2r+1 \pmod{4r+2}$. Andrews proved that $A_r(n)=B_r(n)$ for all positive integers $r$ and nonnegative integers $n$, thereby recovering MacMahon's identity as the special case $r=1$. We derive generating functions and asymptotic formulas for the total numbers of hooks of lengths $1$, $2$, and $3$ in both partition classes, employing Wright's circle method. These asymptotic formulas yield several hook length bias results. In particular, for sufficiently large $n$, the $B_r$-type partitions exhibit more $1$-hooks than the $A_r$-type partitions, whereas for hook lengths $2$ and $3$, the bias is reversed when $r=1$ and favors the $B_r$-type partitions when $r\geq 2$. We also establish asymptotic inequalities among hook numbers of different lengths within each partition class. Finally, for fixed hook lengths $\ell\in\{1,2,3\}$, we prove that the $B_r$-type partitions exhibit more $\ell$-hooks than the $B_{r+1}$-type partitions for sufficiently large $n$. These results reveal systematic asymptotic patterns in the distribution of small hook lengths across the two partition families.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33791
  43. [2610.33990] 三次有理映射模空间的精细点
    Fine points of the moduli space of cubic rational maps
    👥 Tanush Shaska
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究三次有理映射模空间中有理点的精细与粗的判别。
    • 用四元数代数分裂性给出平凡自同构点的精细判据，并处理含对合轨迹。
    • 证明障碍类非分歧、仅在特定余维二轨迹上分歧。
    • 大规模数值普查支持精细点比例按 (log B)^(-1/2) 衰减的猜想。
    💡 给出三次有理映射模空间中精细点的四元数代数判别准则，并分析障碍类的分歧与分布。
    📖 中文摘要

    三次有理映射模空间 M3 的有理点称为精细的，若其共轭类含定义在 Q 上的映射，否则称为粗的。在嵌入 M3 到 P(2,2,3,3,4,6) 的不变量 ξ0,…,ξ5 中，我们证明自同构群平凡的点是精细的当且仅当四元数代数 (-m12,-2ξ0)_Q 分裂，其中 m12=(1/3)ξ0^2ξ1+ξ0ξ4-ξ3^2；我们在含对合的映射轨迹 L2 上给出类似判据，并证明其余层都是精细的。证明是构造性的并给出显式下降。我们证明障碍类沿紧化的每个除子非分歧，素数 p≥5 仅在点模 p 约化落入两个余维二轨迹 L1、L2 闭包之一时构成障碍，且 M3 沿两者有横截 A1 奇点，障碍类在其上分歧；局部密度预测 L2 以约 1/p 的概率被模 p 命中，故它承载障碍。对权模高度不超过 7 的全部 214,260,288 个有理点的普查与该机制一致，并支持精细点比例趋于零如 (log B)^(-1/2) 的猜想。

    🌐 English Abstract

    A rational point of the moduli space $\mathcal{M}_3$ of cubic rational maps is fine if its conjugacy class contains a map defined over $\mathbb{Q}$, and coarse otherwise. In the invariants $\xi_0,\dots,\xi_5$ that embed $\mathcal{M}_3$ in $\mathbb{P}(2,2,3,3,4,6)$, we prove that a point with trivial automorphism group is fine if and only if the quaternion algebra $(-m_{12},-2\xi_0)_{\mathbb{Q}}$ splits, where $m_{12}=\frac{1}{3}\xi_0^2\xi_1+\xi_0\xi_4-\xi_3^2$; we give the analogous criterion on the locus $\mathcal{L}_2$ of maps with an involution and show that all other strata are fine. The proof is constructive and yields an explicit descent. We prove that the obstruction class is unramified along every divisor of the compactification, that a prime $p\ge 5$ obstructs only if the point reduces modulo $p$ into the closure of one of the two codimension-two loci $\mathcal{L}_1$, $\mathcal{L}_2$ of maps with an involution, and that $\mathcal{M}_3$ has transverse $A_1$-singularities along both, above which the class ramifies; local densities predict that $\mathcal{L}_2$ is met modulo $p$ with probability $\asymp 1/p$, so that it carries the obstruction. A census of all 214,260,288 rational points of weighted moduli height at most 7 agrees with this mechanism and supports the conjecture that the proportion of fine points tends to zero like $(\log B)^{-1/2}$.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33990
  44. [2610.34016] 有限域扩域上包含固定子码的 MDS 码
    MDS codes containing a fixed subcode over finite field extensions
    👥 Yangcheng Li, Pingzhi Yuan
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究固定 (k-1) 维子码能否补成扩域上的 MDS 码。
    • 构造辅助线性码，用满权字刻画 MDS 补全。
    • 证明存在性等价于 d(U)≥n-k+1，并用导出拟阵特征多项式给出计数公式。
    • 由此确定最小扩域次数，并揭示计数与拟阵表示的关系。
    💡 刻画固定子码可补成 MDS 码的条件，并用导出拟阵特征多项式给出扩域上计数公式。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 2≤k≤n，U≤F_q^n 是固定的 (k-1) 维线性码。我们研究包含 U 的标量扩张的 F_q 有限扩域上的 [n,k] 最大距离可分（MDS）码。方法是从 U 构造辅助线性码，其满权字刻画所需的 MDS 补全。我们证明 U 在 F_q 的某个有限扩域上存在 [n,k] MDS 补全当且仅当 d(U)≥n-k+1。当条件成立时，F_{q^m} 上这类 MDS 码的个数为 #{W≤F_{q^m}^n : U⊗F_{q^m}⊆W, dim W=k, W 是 MDS} = χ_{δGM(G)}(q^m)/(q^m-1)，其中 G 是 U 的生成矩阵，δGM(G) 是其表示的导出拟阵，χ 是其特征多项式。特别地，该公式确定每个扩域上的存在性及最小扩域次数。例子表明这些计数可区分同一列拟阵的不同表示，而单项不等价的码可能在每个有限扩域上计数相同。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $2\le k\le n$ and let $U\le\mathbb{F}_q^n$ be a fixed $(k-1)$-dimensional linear code. We study the $[n,k]$ maximum distance separable (MDS) codes over finite extensions of $\mathbb{F}_q$ that contain the scalar extension of $U$. Our approach constructs an auxiliary linear code from \(U\). Its full-weight words characterize the desired MDS completions. We prove that \(U\) admits an \([n,k]\) MDS completion over some finite extension of \(\mathbb{F}_q\) if and only if \(d(U)\ge n-k+1\). When this condition holds, the number of these MDS codes over $\mathbb{F}_{q^m}$ is \[\#\left\{W\le\mathbb{F}_{q^m}^n:U\otimes_{\mathbb{F}_q}\mathbb{F}_{q^m}\subseteq W,\ \dim_{\mathbb{F}_{q^m}} W=k,\ W\text{ is MDS}\right\}=\frac{\chi_{\delta_GM(G)}(q^m)}{q^m-1},\] where $G$ is a generator matrix of $U$, $\delta_GM(G)$ is its represented derived matroid, and $\chi_{\delta_GM(G)}$ is its characteristic polynomial. In particular, the formula determines existence over each extension field and the least extension degree for which such an MDS code exists. Examples show that these counts may distinguish different representations of the same column matroid, while monomially inequivalent codes may have identical counts over every finite extension.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34016

    Pointwise ergodic theorems along oscillating sequences
    👥 Leonidas Daskalakis
    🤖 AI 总结
    • 研究沿振荡序列的非传统遍历平均的逐点收敛。
    • 对多项式与周期解析函数复合的相位证明几乎处处收敛。

    • 这是该类问题的首个逐点结果，并覆盖多种具体振荡轨道。
    💡 证明沿一大类振荡序列的非传统遍历平均的逐点收敛，覆盖含 sin、cos 等的具体轨道。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们建立沿一大类振荡序列的非传统遍历平均的首个逐点收敛结果，例如形如 (⌊n^d sin(eπn)⌋) 的轨道，d∈N。具体地，对任意概率空间 (X,B,μ)、可逆保测变换 T:X→X 及 f∈L^p_μ(X)，p∈(1,∞]，我们证明极限 lim_{N→∞} (1/N)Σ_{n=1}^N f(T^{⌊P(n)ψ(αn)⌋}x) 对 μ-几乎处处 x 存在，其中 P 是实系数非常数多项式，ψ 是 1-周期实解析函数，α 的无理性指数小于 1+√(deg(P)+1)。该假设对几乎所有 α∈R 成立，且结果覆盖 ⌊n cos(eπn)⌋、⌊n^37 sin(n)⌋ 等具体轨道。证明采用单频圆法估计指数和，但优弧逼近不取直接连续对应物：在优弧区域将 ψ 振荡部分与多项式部分解耦，用 ∫_0^1 (1/N)∫_0^N e^{2πiξP(t)ψ(s)}dt ds 逼近指数和，这是此类问题的新现象，并用到 Radon 型算子的无系数变分估计。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We establish the first pointwise convergence result for nonconventional ergodic averages modeled along a large class of oscillating sequences, treating, for example, orbits of the form $\big(\lfloor n^d\sin(e\pi n)\rfloor\big)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}$, $d\in\mathbb{N}$. More specifically, for every probability space $(X,\mathcal{B},\mu)$ equipped with an invertible measure-preserving transformation $T\colon X\to X$ and every $f\in L^p_{\mu}(X)$ with $p\in(1,\infty]$, we prove that the limit \[ \lim_{N\to\infty}\frac{1}{N}\sum_{n=1}^Nf\big(T^{\lfloor P(n)\psi(\alpha n)\rfloor}x\big)\quad\text{exists for $\mu$-a.e. $x\in X$,} \] where $P$ is a non-constant polynomial with real coefficients, $\psi$ is $1$-periodic and real-analytic, and $\alpha$ has irrationality exponent smaller than $1+\sqrt{\operatorname{deg}(P)+1}$. On the one hand, the latter assumption is satisfied by a.e. $\alpha\in\mathbb{R}$, and, on the other hand, our result covers any of the following concrete orbits \[ \lfloor n\cos(e\pi n)\rfloor\text{,}\quad\lfloor n^{37}\sin(n)\rfloor\text{,}\quad\big\lfloor \sqrt{2}n^{10}e^{\sin(\pi (\sqrt[5]{7}+1) n)}\big\rfloor\text{,}\quad \big\lfloor \big((\log 2) n^3+e n\big)e^{e^{\cos(\pi \sqrt{2} n)}}\big\rfloor\text{.} \] The proof employs the so-called one-frequency circle method for estimating exponential sums with phases $\big(\xi \lfloor P(n)\psi(\alpha n)\rfloor\big)_{1\le n\le N}$, but the major arc approximants of our exponential sums do not consist of their direct continuous counterparts. In the major arc regime we decouple the $\psi$-oscillatory part of the phase from the polynomial one, and approximate the exponential sum by $\int_0^1\big(\frac{1}{N}\int_0^Ne^{2\pi i\xi P(t)\psi( s)}dt\big)ds$. This represents a novel phenomenon for problems of this kind, and, interestingly, our proof exploits the coefficient-free variational estimates of Radon-type operators.

    💡 证明经 Schwarz 反射延拓的模 j 函数满足模存在封闭性猜想，用 Ratner 定理与 Ax-Schanuel 处理非超定方程组。
    📖 中文摘要

    我们证明经 Schwarz 反射从上半平面延拓到 C\R 的模 j 函数的模存在封闭性猜想。具体地，我们证明任何涉及域运算与 j 的方程组在复数中都有解，除非它是超定的。粗略地说，若独立方程数多于变量数（可能在应用 j 在 PGL2(Q) 分式线性作用下的变换规则后），则方程组超定。由于模 Schanuel 猜想所刻画的 j 的代数与超越性质，一般不应期望超定方程组有解。几何地，模存在封闭性断言：给定宽且自由的不可约代数簇 V⊆C^n×C^n（宽与自由是保证非超定的几何条件），存在 (τ1,…,τn,j(τ1),…,j(τn))∈V 且 τi∈C\R。证明用 Ratner 关于 PGL2(R) 幂上单幂流轨道闭包与等分布定理描述某些解析映射轨道的渐近行为，再用原方程组与 PGL2(Z) 作用逼近较简单的方程组。极限系统的构造用到 Ax-Schanuel 定理并保证孤立解，最后用 Rouché 定理得到原方程组的解。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We prove the Modular Existential Closedness conjecture for the modular $j$-function extended from the upper half-plane to $\mathbb{C} \setminus \mathbb{R}$ by Schwarz reflection. Specifically, we show that any system of equations involving the field operations and $j$ has solutions in the complex numbers unless it is overdetermined. Such a system is overdetermined if, roughly, there are more independent equations than variables, possibly after applying the transformation rules of the $j$-function under the linear fractional action of $\rm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{Q})$ on $\mathbb{C} \setminus \mathbb{R}$. In general, we do not expect overdetermined systems of equations to have solutions due to the algebraic and transcendental properties of the $j$-function captured by the modular analogue of Schanuel's conjecture. In geometric terms, Modular Existential Closedness states that given a broad and free irreducible algebraic variety $V \subseteq \mathbb{C}^n \times \mathbb{C}^n$ -- where broadness and freeness are geometric conditions guaranteeing that the system is not overdetermined -- there is a point of the form $(\tau_1, \ldots, \tau_n, j(\tau_1), \ldots, j(\tau_n)) \in V$ with $\tau_i \in \mathbb{C} \setminus \mathbb{R}$. The proof uses Ratner's theorems on the closure and equidistribution of the orbits of unipotent flows in powers of $\rm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{R})$ to describe the asymptotic behaviour of the orbits of certain analytic maps. This is then applied to approximate a relatively simple system of equations using the original system and the action of $\rm{PGL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$. The construction of the limiting system makes use of the Ax--Schanuel theorem and guarantees an isolated solution. Rouch\'e's theorem then establishes the existence of solutions for the original system of equations.

    👉 https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35409

    💡 证明 n≡2 mod 4、n≥6、r≥4 时 C_n^r 的性质 C 与 D 均失效，并给出 s 的下界。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 s(C_n^r) 是 Erdős-Ginzburg-Ziv 常数（长度恰为 n 的零和），η(C_n^r) 是短零和常数。Gao 等人的性质 C 与 D 断言：每个不含相应零和子序列的极长序列都具有形式 T^{n-1}。对偶数 n，它们蕴含 s(C_n^r)=2^r(n-1)+1 与 η(C_n^r)=(2^r-1)(n-1)+1。G. Wang 最近对 C_6^r（r≥4）否证了两者。我们证明对每个 n≡2 mod 4、n≥6 及每个 r≥4，两者对 C_n^r 都失效。更精确地，对这些 n 有 s(C_n^r)≥2^r(n-1)+2^{r-2}+1。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $s(C_n^r)$ be the Erd\H{o}s--Ginzburg--Ziv constant (zero-sums of length exactly $n$) and $\eta(C_n^r)$ the short zero-sum constant. Properties C and D of Gao et al.\ assert that every sequence of the extremal length without the relevant zero-sums has the form $T^{n-1}$. For even $n$ they imply $s(C_n^r)=2^r(n-1)+1$ and $\eta(C_n^r)=(2^r-1)(n-1)+1$. G.~Wang recently disproved both for $C_6^r$, $r\ge4$. We prove that both fail for $C_n^r$ for every $n\equiv2\pmod4$ with $n\ge6$ and every $r\ge4$. More precisely, for these $n$ we have: $s(C_n^r)\ge2^r(n-1)+2^{r-2}+1$.

We also study (semi)perfectoid rings and perfectoid ideals and take the opportunity to make some structural remarks about them.

    📖 中文摘要

    我们研究离散化区间上的景观函数。离散景观有显式闭式，其谱系数可精确计算。我们证明这些系数位于 Q 的显式阿贝尔扩域中，并对每个奇数 k 得到界 [Q(c_k(N)):Q]≤φ(N)。Chandra-Jain 猜想断言对所有 N≥3 有 [Q(c_1(N)):Q]=φ(N)，该猜想已由 Q. Zhou 证明（Zenodo, doi:10.5281/zenodo.21935814）。我们随后将高模态化归为互素情形。对 3≤N≤50 的全部 624 对精确计算显示，非互素模态只出现次数 φ(N/d) 与 2φ(N/d)，其中 d=gcd(N,k)。这引出第二 Chandra-Jain 猜想：该次数二分性普遍成立。我们还讨论与奇偶性、Arnold 猫映射及 Lefschetz 数的联系。

    🌐 English Abstract

    We study the landscape function on a discretized interval. The discrete landscape has an explicit closed form, and its spectral coefficients can be computed exactly. We show that these coefficients lie in explicit abelian extensions of $\mathbb{Q}$ and obtain the bound $$[\mathbb{Q}(c_k(N)):\mathbb{Q}]\leq\varphi(N)$$ for every odd $k$. The Chandra--Jain conjecture, asserting that $[\mathbb{Q}(c_1(N)):\mathbb{Q}]=\varphi(N)$ for all $N\geq3$, has since been proved by Q. Zhou (Zenodo, doi:10.5281/zenodo.21935814). We then reduce higher modes to the coprime case. Exact computations for all 624 pairs with $3\leq N\leq50$ reveal only the degrees $\varphi(N/d)$ and $2\varphi(N/d)$ for non-coprime modes, where $d=\gcd(N,k)$. This motivates the Second Chandra--Jain Conjecture that this degree dichotomy holds generally. We also discuss connections with parity, the Arnold cat map, and Lefschetz numbers.

    👥 Guoqing Wang, Xiangneng Zeng

    💡 证明对 r≥4 与偶数 n，性质 C、D 成立当且仅当 n 是 2 的幂，否定相关猜想。
    📖 中文摘要

    设 C_n^r 表示 r 个 n 阶循环群的直和，n≥2，r≥1。性质 C 与 D 预测 C_n^r 上极值零和问题的统一重数结构：每个不含长度至多 n 的非空零和子序列（或长度恰为 n 的零和子序列）的最长序列都应具有形式 T^{n-1}。我们证明对每个 r≥4 与每个偶数 n≥2，这两个性质各自成立当且仅当 n 是 2 的幂。这否定了 Gao、Hou、Schmid 与 Thangadurai 于 2007 年提出的性质 C 一般问题（Open Problems 1.2），并否证了 Gao 与 Geroldinger 于 2006 年提出的性质 D 猜想（Conjecture 7.2）。

    🌐 English Abstract

    Let $C_n^r$ denote the direct sum of $r$ cyclic groups of order $n$, where $n\ge2$ and $r\ge1$. Properties C and D predict a uniform multiplicity structure for extremal zero-sum problems over $C_n^r$: every sequence of maximum length avoiding nonempty zero-sum subsequences of length at most $n$, or of length exactly $n$, respectively, should have the form $T^{n-1}$. We prove that, for every $r\ge4$ and every even $n\ge2$, each of these properties holds if and only if $n$ is a power of two. This gives negative answers to the general Property C problem posed by Gao, Hou, Schmid and Thangadurai Open Problems 1.2{GHST} in 2007, and disproves the Property D conjecture proposed by Gao and Geroldinger [Conjecture 7.2]{GG} in 2006.
